                            error("car spawn timer" .. (Travel.timerLeft() > 0 and (" (" .. Travel.timerLeft() .. "s)") or "") .. (Travel.feedback ~= "" and (": " .. Travel.feedback) or ""))
                        end
                        error("Car didn't spawn" .. (Travel.feedback ~= "" and (" (" .. Travel.feedback .. ")") or ". Spawn one normally once so the script learns how"))
                    end
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
                -- get in (spawn protection can block it for a bit -> wait, bring the car over, try again)
                local inCar = getIn(car)
                local tries = 1
                while not inCar and tries < 6 and car.Parent do
                    if Travel._cancelWait then error("you pressed Stop") end
                    if L:GetAttribute("SpawnProtected") == true or Travel.inSafeZone() then
                        notifyFn("Travel", "Spawn protection — waiting a moment, then getting in the car…", "clock")
                        if Travel.inSafeZone() then pcall(Travel.leaveSafeZone, target, notifyFn) end
                        local tw = os.clock()
                        while L:GetAttribute("SpawnProtected") == true and os.clock() - tw < 20 do
                            if Travel._cancelWait then error("you pressed Stop") end
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end
                    runToCar(car)
                    task.wait(0.6)
                    tries = tries + 1
                    inCar = getIn(car)
                end
                if not inCar then
                    Travel.badCount = (Travel.badCar == car and (Travel.badCount or 0) or 0) + 1
                    Travel.badCar = car
                    error("Couldn't get in the car")
                end
                Travel.badCount = 0
                if opts and opts.noDrive then result, rmsg = true, ""; return end   -- just get in, don't drive anywhere
                if label then notifyFn("Travel", "Driving to " .. label .. "…", "navigation") end
                local finished = false
                -- stop NEXT to the place on the street (never on a roof), then glide the last bit
                local driveTarget = target
                if hopOut and not (opts and opts.noCurb) then
                    local okC, curb = pcall(Travel.curbPoint, target, origin)
                    if okC and curb then driveTarget = curb end
                end
                if Travel.pierClamp then driveTarget = Travel.pierClamp(driveTarget) end   -- 🔒 never into the pier safe zone
                driveTo(car, driveTarget, function(ok, msg)
                    if not ok and msg == "STUCK" then
                        -- ran into a wall: hop out, car waits underground, noclip straight to the place
                        notifyFn("Travel", "Wall in the way — going through it on foot…", "footprints")
                        Travel.exitCar()
                        local arrived = Travel.glide and Travel.glide(target, function() return not Travel._cancelWait end)
                        finished = true
                        result, rmsg = arrived and true or false, arrived and "" or "couldn't get through"
                        return
                    end
                    result, rmsg = ok, msg or ""
                    if ok and hopOut then
                        task.wait(0.3)
                        Travel.exitCar()
                        Travel.park()
                        -- the car stopped NEXT to the place (not on its roof) -> glide the last bit
                        if driveTarget ~= target and Travel.glide then
                            pcall(Travel.glide, target + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), function() return not Travel._cancelWait end)
                        end
                    end
                    finished = true
                end, opts and { above = opts.above, direct = opts.direct } or nil)
                while not finished do
                    task.wait(0.25)
                    -- you died mid-drive -> end this trip so the farm can start again after respawn
                    local h = tripChar and tripChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if L.Character ~= tripChar or not h or h.Health <= 0 then
                        Travel.stop()
                        task.wait(0.3)
                        if not finished then finished = true end
                        result, rmsg = false, "you died"
                    end
                end
            end)
            busy = false
            if not okAll then result, rmsg = false, (string.gsub(tostring(err), "^.-:%d+: ", "")) end
            if Travel._died then Travel._died = false; result, rmsg = false, "you died" end
            if onFinish then pcall(onFinish, result, rmsg) end
        end)
    end
    -- drive the car (you must already be in it) through a list of points, above ground,
    -- straight from point to point (used for the dock ramp)
    function Travel.drivePath(points, notifyFn, usePath)
        local car = Travel.myCar()
        if not car or not seatedIn(car) then return false, "not in the car" end
        if busy then return false, "Already driving" end
        busy = true
        Travel._cancelWait = false
        local ok, msg = true, ""
        for _, p in ipairs(points) do
            if Travel._cancelWait then ok, msg = false, "you pressed Stop"; break end
            local fin, o2, m2 = false, false, ""
            driveTo(car, p, function(a, b) o2, m2, fin = a, b, true end, { above = true, direct = true, noDodge = true })
            local t0 = os.clock()
            local maxT = 60
            while not fin do
                task.wait(0.1)
                if Travel._cancelWait or os.clock() - t0 > maxT then finish(false, Travel._cancelWait and "you pressed Stop" or "STUCK") end
            end
            if not o2 then ok, msg = false, tostring(m2); break end
        end
        busy = false
        return ok, msg
    end
    -- drive the car along fixed points at your normal driving speed (70) — the dock ramp / dock.
    -- No dodging, no slowing down, no pathfinding: it follows the points exactly and sticks to the ground.
    -- opts.exact(a, b) -> true = drive that piece in a straight line at exact heights (no ground
    -- following, collisions off) — used to drive INTO the rock at the fishing spot
    function Travel.rampDrive(points, notifyFn, opts)
        if Travel.pierClamp and not (opts and opts.allowZone) then   -- (the quick sell drive is the only exception)
            local out = {}
            for _, pt in ipairs(points) do
                local q, c = Travel.pierClamp(pt)
                out[#out + 1] = q
                if c then break end                -- stop AT the park spot, never past it
            end
            points = out
        end
        local car = Travel.myCar()
        if not car or not seatedIn(car) then return false, "not in the car" end
        if busy then return false, "Already driving" end
        if #points == 0 then return true, "" end
        busy = true
        Travel._cancelWait = false
        local okC, cf = pcall(car.GetPivot, car)
        if not okC then busy = false; return false, "Car has no position" end
        local rp = RaycastParams.new()
        rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        local ign = { car, L.Character }
        for _, p in ipairs(P:GetPlayers()) do if p.Character then ign[#ign + 1] = p.Character end end
        rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = ign
        pcall(function() rp.RespectCanCollide = true end)
        local function ground(x, yTop, z)
            local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(x, yTop, z), Vector3.new(0, -40, 0), rp)
            return hit and hit.Position.Y or nil
        end
        local function flat(a, b) return Vector3.new(a.X - b.X, 0, a.Z - b.Z).Magnitude end
        local pos = cf.Position
        local g0 = ground(pos.X, pos.Y + 4, pos.Z)
        local hover = g0 and math.clamp(pos.Y - g0, 1.5, 6) or 3
        -- skip points that are already behind the car
        local i = 1
        while i < #points and flat(points[i + 1], pos) < flat(points[i + 1], points[i]) do i = i + 1 end
        local from = pos
        local total = flat(pos, points[i])
        for k = i + 1, #points do total = total + flat(points[k], points[k - 1]) end
        local RAMP_SPEED = math.min(S.DriveSpeed, 70)   -- ramp / dock: steady 70
        local maxT = total / math.max(RAMP_SPEED, 10) + 15
        local t0 = os.clock()
        local done, res, msg = false, true, ""
        local rotNow = cf.Rotation
        Travel.driving = true
        Travel.camDrive(true)
        local exactFn = opts and opts.exact
        local ghosted = false
        local zoneAtStart = Travel.carZoneHit()
        local allowZone = opts and opts.allowZone           -- driving INTO a safe zone on purpose (the fish seller)
        local stopOut = opts and opts.stopOut               -- stop as soon as we're out of the safe zone
        local outAcc = 0
        Travel.zoneIn = zoneAtStart and os.clock() or nil
        local backing, backDir, backExtra = false, nil, 0
        local lastDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
        local conn
        conn = RunS.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if done then return end
            if Travel._cancelWait then done, res, msg = true, false, "you pressed Stop"; return end
            if not car.Parent or not seatedIn(car) then done, res, msg = true, false, "not in the car"; return end
            if os.clock() - t0 > maxT + (backing and 10 or 0) then done, res, msg = true, false, "STUCK"; return end
            -- cars get REMOVED in a safe zone -> the moment we touch one, reverse out of it and stop there
            if allowZone then
                if Travel.carZoneHit() then
                    Travel.zoneIn = Travel.zoneIn or os.clock()
                elseif stopOut then
                    outAcc = outAcc + RAMP_SPEED * dt
                    if outAcc >= 9 then done, res, msg = true, true, "out"; return end
                end
            end
            if not allowZone and not backing and not zoneAtStart and Travel.carZoneHit() then
                backing, backDir = true, -lastDir
                if Travel.trace then pcall(Travel.trace, "car touched a safe zone -> backing out") end
            end
            if backing then
                if not Travel.carZoneHit() then backExtra = backExtra + 30 * dt end
                pos = pos + backDir * 30 * dt
                local gyB = ground(pos.X, pos.Y + 4, pos.Z)
                if gyB and math.abs(gyB + hover - pos.Y) < 4 then pos = Vector3.new(pos.X, gyB + hover, pos.Z) end
                pcall(function()
                    car:PivotTo(CFrame.new(pos) * rotNow)
                    local pp = car.PrimaryPart or driverSeat(car)
                    if pp then pp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero; pp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
                end)
                if backExtra >= 10 then done, res, msg = true, true, "safezone" end
                return
            end
            local step = RAMP_SPEED * dt
            local dir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            while step > 0 do
                local wp = points[i]
                local f = Vector3.new(wp.X - pos.X, 0, wp.Z - pos.Z)
                local d = f.Magnitude
                if d > step then
                    dir = f.Unit
                    pos = pos + dir * step
                    step = 0
                else
                    if d > 0.01 then dir = f.Unit end
                    pos = Vector3.new(wp.X, pos.Y, wp.Z)
                    step = step - d
                    from = wp
                    i = i + 1
                    if i > #points then done = true; break end
                end
            end
            -- height: the ground under the car (searching down from the route's height), else the route's height
            local wp = points[math.min(i, #points)]
            local segLen = flat(wp, from)
            local tt = segLen > 0.1 and math.clamp(1 - flat(wp, pos) / segLen, 0, 1) or 1
            local lineY = from.Y + (wp.Y - from.Y) * tt
            local y
            if exactFn and exactFn(from, wp) then
                if not ghosted then ghosted = true; pcall(carCollide, car, false) end   -- into the rock
                y = lineY
            else
                local gy = ground(pos.X, math.max(lineY, pos.Y) + 4, pos.Z)
                y = gy and (gy + hover) or lineY
                if y > lineY + hover + 4 then y = lineY + hover end   -- something above the pier: go through, not over
            end
            pos = Vector3.new(pos.X, y, pos.Z)
            if dir.Magnitude > 0.5 then lastDir = dir end
            if dir.Magnitude > 0.5 then rotNow = rotNow:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + dir).Rotation, math.clamp(dt * 8, 0, 1)) end
            pcall(function()
                car:PivotTo(CFrame.new(pos) * rotNow)
                local pp = car.PrimaryPart or driverSeat(car)
                if pp then
                    pp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = done and Vector3.zero or dir * RAMP_SPEED * 0.2
                    pp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end)
        end)
        while not done do task.wait(0.05) end
        conn:Disconnect()
        Travel.driving = false
        if ghosted and not (exactFn and exactFn(points[math.max(#points - 1, 1)], points[#points])) then pcall(carCollide, car, true) end
        pcall(function()
            local pp = car.PrimaryPart or driverSeat(car)
            if pp then pp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero; pp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
        end)
        busy = false
        return res, msg
    end
    function Travel.hopOut()
        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum and hum.SeatPart then Travel.exitCar(); pcall(Travel.park) end
    end
    function Travel.go(key, notifyFn)
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        if busy then return notifyFn("Travel", "Already on the way — press Stop first", "x") end
        local place
        for _, p in ipairs(PLACES) do if p.key == key then place = p end end
        local target = Travel.placePos(key)
        if not target then
            return notifyFn("Travel", "Couldn't find the " .. place.name .. ". Stand at it and press \"Set " .. place.name .. " Here\"", "x")
        end
        Travel.trip(target, "the " .. place.name, notifyFn, S.HopOut, function(ok, msg)
            if ok then notifyFn("Travel", "Arrived at the " .. place.name .. " " .. msg, "check")
            else notifyFn("Travel", "Stopped: " .. msg, "x") end
        end, { waitAll = true })
    end
    function Travel.cancel() Travel._cancelWait = true; Travel.stop() end
    -- died -> stop the old trip right away (the farm loop drives again after you respawn)
    L.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        if busy or Travel.driving then Travel._died = true; Travel._cancelWait = true; Travel.stop() end
        Travel.hiddenCar = nil
    end)

    task.delay(3, function() Travel.scan() end)
end

-- ================================================================
-- BOX FARM  (Delivery Job at the gas station — built from your box dump)
--   Pickup : workspace.PickupBox / PickupBoxGolden / PickupBoxEsmerald  -> "PickupPrompt"
--   Then   : you get a Box Tool, workspace.BoxJobPrivateRing (blue circle) appears,
--            DeliveryState {action="target"} -> stand on it -> {action="start", duration=5}
--            -> 5s later {action="complete"} (+cash / xp)
--   Gold + Green have timers (their prompt is off while waiting). 5 gold = triple cash.
--   Noclip is ON while Auto Farm is on, OFF when it's off.
-- ================================================================
local Farm = {}
do
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local RunS = game:GetService("RunService")
    local jobR = RS:FindFirstChild("BoxJobRemotes")

    local STATIONS = {
        { name = "PickupBoxEsmerald", kind = "green",  tool = "EsmeralBox" },
        { name = "PickupBoxGolden",   kind = "gold",   tool = "GoldenBox" },
        { name = "PickupBox",         kind = "normal", tool = "Box" },
    }
    local banned = {}
    Farm.status, Farm.buff, Farm.lastNotify = "Off", "", ""
    local deliveryAction, deliveryAt = "", 0

    local function station(st)
        local m = workspace:FindFirstChild(st.name)
        if not m then m = workspace:FindFirstChild(st.name, true) end
        return m
    end
    local function stationPrompt(m)
        if not m then return nil end
        return m:FindFirstChild("PickupPrompt", true) or m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
    end
    local function stationPos(m)
        if m:IsA("BasePart") then return m.Position end
        local ok, cf = pcall(m.GetPivot, m)
        return ok and cf.Position or nil
    end
    local function timerText(m)
        for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("TextLabel") then
                local t = d.Text
                if string.match(t, "%d+:%d%d") or string.match(t, "%d+%s*[sm]%f[%A]") then return t end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function ready(st)
        if banned[st.name] and banned[st.name] > os.clock() then return false end
        local m = station(st)
        local pr = stationPrompt(m)
        return pr ~= nil and pr.Enabled
    end

    function Farm.counts()
        local c = {}
        for _, st in ipairs(STATIONS) do
            local m = station(st)
            local e = { r = ready(st) and 1 or 0, t = m and 1 or 0, timer = nil }
            if m and e.r == 0 then e.timer = timerText(m) end
            c[st.kind] = e
        end
        return c
    end

    -- ── carrying / ring ──────────────────────────────────────────
    local function boxTool()
        local char = L.Character
        for _, st in ipairs(STATIONS) do
            local t = (char and char:FindFirstChild(st.tool))
            if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return t, true end
        end
        local bp = L:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if bp then
            for _, st in ipairs(STATIONS) do
                local t = bp:FindFirstChild(st.tool)
                if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return t, false end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function isCarrying() return boxTool() ~= nil end
    local KIND_OF_TOOL = {}
    for _, st in ipairs(STATIONS) do KIND_OF_TOOL[st.tool] = st.kind end
    Farm.delivered = { green = 0, gold = 0, normal = 0 }
    Farm.quota = nil      -- { green = n, gold = n, normal = n } while Both Jobs runs
    Farm.isCarrying = isCarrying
    local function equipBox()
        local t, equipped = boxTool()
        if t and not equipped then
            local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then pcall(hum.EquipTool, hum, t) end
        end
    end
    local function ringPart()
        local ring = workspace:FindFirstChild("BoxJobPrivateRing")
        if not ring then return nil end
        if ring:IsA("BasePart") then return ring end
        return ring:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    end

    -- ── remotes ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if jobR then
        local ds = jobR:FindFirstChild("DeliveryState")
        if ds then ds.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(data)
            if type(data) == "table" and type(data.action) == "string" then
                deliveryAction, deliveryAt = data.action, os.clock()
            end
        end) end
        local gb = jobR:FindFirstChild("GoldenBuffState")
        if gb then gb.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(d)
            if type(d) == "table" then
                if d.active then
                    Farm.buff = string.format("🔥 %sx cash (%s) %ds left", tostring(d.multiplier or "?"), tostring(d.kind or ""), tonumber(d.remainingSeconds) or 0)
                else
                    Farm.buff = string.format("%s boxes: %s/%s for %sx cash", tostring(d.kind or "Golden"), tostring(d.count or 0), tostring(d.needed or 5), tostring(d.multiplier or 3))
                end
            end
        end) end
        local jn = jobR:FindFirstChild("JobNotify")
        if jn then jn.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(a) Farm.lastNotify = type(a) == "table" and tostring(a.text or a.message or "") or tostring(a) end) end
    end

    -- ── noclip (only while farming) ──────────────────────────────
    local ncConn, ncSaved = nil, {}
    local function setNoclip(on)
        if not on then return end   -- noclip is ALWAYS on in this script
        if on then
            if ncConn then return end
            ncConn = RunS.Stepped:Connect(function()
                local char = L.Character
                if not char then return end
                -- ONLY while you sit in the car.  On foot you collide like a normal player:
                -- the game's server pulls you back if your body goes through things on your screen only.
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if Farm.noclipPaused or not (hum and hum.SeatPart) then
                    if next(ncSaved) then
                        for p in pairs(ncSaved) do if p.Parent then p.CanCollide = true end end
                        ncSaved = {}
                    end
                    return
                end
                for _, p in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.CanCollide then
                        if ncSaved[p] == nil then ncSaved[p] = true end
                        p.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if ncConn then ncConn:Disconnect(); ncConn = nil end
            for p in pairs(ncSaved) do if p.Parent then p.CanCollide = true end end
            ncSaved = {}
        end
        if Farm.onNoclip then pcall(Farm.onNoclip, on) end
    end
    Farm.setNoclip = setNoclip
    function Farm.pauseNoclip(on)
        Farm.noclipPaused = on and true or false
        if on then
            for p in pairs(ncSaved) do if p.Parent then p.CanCollide = true end end
            ncSaved = {}
        end
    end
    function Farm.killNoclip()
        if ncConn then ncConn:Disconnect(); ncConn = nil end
        for p in pairs(ncSaved) do if p.Parent then p.CanCollide = true end end
        ncSaved = {}
    end
    setNoclip(true)   -- on from the moment you execute, stays on

    -- ── on foot: SPRINT ONLY (locked — no gliding) ───────────────
    --   you really run there (MoveTo) at your Sprint Speed; if something blocks
    --   the way it jumps, then walks a real path around it.
    local PFS2 = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
    -- 0 = the game's own sprint speed.  A number only ever makes you SLOWER than the game's
    -- speed — going faster than the server allows is what pulled you back every few studs.
    local function sprintSpeed() return 16 end   -- 🔒 locked at 16
    local function setSprint(on)
        pcall(function() L:SetAttribute("SprintRequested", on); L:SetAttribute("MobileSprint", on) end)
    end
    Farm.setSprint = setSprint
    -- "ok" | "stuck" | "fell" | "stop"
    local function runDirect(hrp, hum, pos, keep, maxT, tol, noJump)
        local function fd() return Vector3.new(pos.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, pos.Z - hrp.Position.Z).Magnitude end
        local t0 = os.clock()
        local chkT, chkD, jumps = os.clock(), fd(), 0
        while true do
            if not (keep() and ALIVE() and hrp.Parent and hum.Parent and hum.Health > 0) then return "stop" end
            local d = fd()
            if d < tol then return "ok" end
            hum:MoveTo(Vector3.new(pos.X, hrp.Position.Y, pos.Z))
            if os.clock() < (SESSION.jumpUntil or 0) + 0.5 then   -- pulled back -> jumping out, not "stuck"
                chkT, chkD = os.clock(), d
                t0 = t0 + 0.1
            end
            if hrp.Position.Y < pos.Y - 12 then return "fell" end
            if os.clock() - chkT >= 1.2 then
                if chkD - d < 1.5 then
                    jumps = jumps + 1
                    if jumps > 2 then return "stuck" end
                    if not noJump then hum.Jump = true end    -- small obstacle -> jump it (never on a trail)
                end
                chkT, chkD = os.clock(), d
            end
            if os.clock() - t0 > maxT then return "stuck" end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end
    -- straight line clear?  no wall in the way (doors are open = fine) AND solid ground the whole way
    -- (no water, no drop off the edge of the pier / a ledge)
    local lineRP = RaycastParams.new()
    lineRP.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    pcall(function() lineRP.RespectCanCollide = true end)
    local groundRP = RaycastParams.new()
    groundRP.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    groundRP.IgnoreWater = false
    local function refreshLineRP()
        local ign = {}
        for _, pl in ipairs(P:GetPlayers()) do
            if pl.Character then ign[#ign + 1] = pl.Character end
        end
        lineRP.FilterDescendantsInstances = ign
        groundRP.FilterDescendantsInstances = ign
    end
    local function groundBelow(x, z, fromY)
        local g = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(x, fromY, z), Vector3.new(0, -16, 0), groundRP)
        if not g then return nil end
        if g.Instance == workspace.Terrain and g.Material == Enum.Material.Water then return nil end
        return g.Position.Y
    end
    local function clearLine(a, b)
        refreshLineRP()
        local flat = Vector3.new(b.X - a.X, 0, b.Z - a.Z)
        local d = flat.Magnitude
        if d < 1 then return true end
        for _, h in ipairs({ -1.6, 0, 1.4 }) do
            if workspace:Raycast(a + Vector3.new(0, h, 0), flat, lineRP) then return false end
        end
        local lastG = groundBelow(a.X, a.Z, a.Y + 1)
        if not lastG then return false end
        local u = flat.Unit
        for st = 2.5, d, 2.5 do
            local q = a + u * st
            local g = groundBelow(q.X, q.Z, lastG + 4)
            if not g or math.abs(g - lastG) > 2.2 then return false end   -- water / edge / ledge
            lastG = g
        end
        return true
    end
    Farm.clearLine = clearLine
    local function makePath(jump)
        return PFS2:CreatePath({ AgentRadius = 2, AgentHeight = 5, AgentCanJump = jump, AgentCanClimb = false,
            WaypointSpacing = 4, Costs = { Water = math.huge } })
    end
    local function computeWalk(from, to)
        for _, jump in ipairs({ false, true }) do              -- no jumping first (around the pier edge, not over it)
            local path = makePath(jump)
            local okP = pcall(function() path:ComputeAsync(from, to) end)
            if okP and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
                local wps = path:GetWaypoints()
                if #wps > 0 then return wps end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function runTo(pos, keep)
        local char = L.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not (hrp and hum) then return false end
        if hum.SeatPart then if Travel.exitCar then Travel.exitCar() else hum.Sit = false; hum.Jump = true; task.wait(0.4) end end
        -- walks EXACTLY like a normal player: real collisions (through doors, not walls), the game's
        -- own speed (no forced sprint / speed changes) and no teleporting.  Forcing those made the
        -- server pull you back every half second (others saw you walking in place).
        local wasPaused = Farm.noclipPaused
        Farm.pauseNoclip(true)
        Farm.walking = true
        local runSpeed = math.max(hum.WalkSpeed, 8)
        SESSION.autoWalk = (SESSION.autoWalk or 0) + 1     -- pull-back watcher may jump you out of it
        local function flat(a) return Vector3.new(pos.X - a.X, 0, pos.Z - a.Z).Magnitude end
        local r = "stuck"
        if flat(hrp.Position) < 6 or clearLine(hrp.Position, pos) then
            -- nothing in the way -> just walk there
            r = runDirect(hrp, hum, pos, keep, flat(hrp.Position) / (runSpeed * 0.6) + 6, 1.8, true)
        end
        -- follow the real walkway: through doors, around walls, along the pier (never jumps the edge)
        -- stuck twice -> it starts jumping to get out of it
        local tries, stucks = 0, (r == "stuck" or r == "fell") and 1 or 0
        while (r == "stuck" or r == "fell") and tries < 3 do
            tries = tries + 1
            local jumpy = stucks >= 2
            if jumpy then hum.Jump = true end
            local wps = computeWalk(hrp.Position, pos)
            if not wps then break end
            r = "ok"
            for k, wp in ipairs(wps) do
                if k > 1 or flat(wp.Position) < flat(hrp.Position) then
                    if wp.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then hum.Jump = true end
                    local seg = Vector3.new(wp.Position.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, wp.Position.Z - hrp.Position.Z).Magnitude
                    local r2 = runDirect(hrp, hum, wp.Position, keep, seg / (runSpeed * 0.5) + 3, 2, not jumpy)
                    if r2 == "stuck" or r2 == "fell" then
                        stucks = stucks + 1
                        jumpy = stucks >= 2
                        if jumpy then
                            -- keep pushing the SAME way, jumping (no turning back)
                            hum.Jump = true
                            local seg2 = Vector3.new(wp.Position.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, wp.Position.Z - hrp.Position.Z).Magnitude
                            r2 = runDirect(hrp, hum, wp.Position, keep, seg2 / (runSpeed * 0.5) + 4, 2, false)
                        end
                    end
                    if r2 == "stop" then r = "stop"; break end
                    if r2 ~= "ok" then r = "stuck"; break end   -- blocked -> work out the way again from here
                end
            end
            if r == "ok" and flat(hrp.Position) > 1.8 then
                r = runDirect(hrp, hum, pos, keep, flat(hrp.Position) / (runSpeed * 0.5) + 3, 1.8, stucks < 2)
            end
        end
        if r == "stuck" or r == "fell" then
            -- no walkway found -> straight there (last resort)
            r = runDirect(hrp, hum, pos, keep, flat(hrp.Position) / (runSpeed * 0.6) + 6, 1.8)
            if r ~= "ok" and r ~= "stop" then r = flat(hrp.Position) < 3 and "ok" or "stuck" end
        end
        SESSION.autoWalk = math.max((SESSION.autoWalk or 1) - 1, 0)
        Farm.walking = false
        Farm.pauseNoclip(wasPaused)
        pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(hrp.Position) end)
        return r == "ok"
    end

    -- (name kept so every farm still calls it — it RUNS now, it never glides)
    function Farm.glideTo(pos, keep)
        keep = keep or function() return S.BoxFarm or S.MopFarm end
        return runTo(pos, keep)
    end
    -- straight down / up through the floor — ONLY for the Underground Fishing option
    function Farm.sinkTo(pos, keep)
        keep = keep or function() return true end
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return false end
        local done = false
        local conn
        conn = RunS.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if not keep() or not hrp.Parent or not ALIVE() then done = true; conn:Disconnect(); return end
            local cur = hrp.Position
            local d = pos - cur
            if d.Magnitude < 0.5 then done = true; conn:Disconnect(); return end
            SESSION.tp(hrp, CFrame.new(cur + d.Unit * math.min(16 * dt, d.Magnitude)) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation, "sinkTo")
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
        while not done do task.wait() end
        return (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude < 2
    end

    -- the gas station (box job).  Car parks here, then you glide to the boxes.
    local GAS_FALLBACK = Vector3.new(-492, 362, 575)
    local function gasPoint()
        local m = workspace:FindFirstChild("PickupBox")
        local p = m and stationPos(m)
        if p then return p + Vector3.new(4, 0, 18) end
        return GAS_FALLBACK
    end
    Farm.gasPoint = gasPoint
    Travel.glide = Farm.glideTo
    local function needDrive()
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp or not S.FarmUseCar or not Travel then return false end
        return (hrp.Position - gasPoint()).Magnitude > S.FarmCarDist
    end
    local function driveToGas(notifyFn)
        Farm.status = "🚗 Driving to the gas station (" .. ("above ground") .. ")…"
        local okTrip, msgTrip = false, ""
        for attempt = 1, 3 do
            local fin = false
            local m = workspace:FindFirstChild("PickupBox")
            local boxAt = m and stationPos(m)
            Travel.trip(boxAt or gasPoint(), nil, notifyFn, true, function(o, m2) okTrip, msgTrip, fin = o, m2, true end)
            while not fin do
                if not S.BoxFarm then Travel.cancel() end
                task.wait(0.25)
            end
            if string.find(tostring(msgTrip), "died", 1, true) then return false end   -- respawn -> the loop drives again
            if okTrip or not S.BoxFarm or string.find(tostring(msgTrip), "Stop", 1, true) then break end
            notifyFn("Farm", "Car trip failed (" .. tostring(msgTrip) .. ") — trying the car again (" .. attempt .. "/3)", "car")
            task.wait(1.5)
        end
        if not okTrip and S.BoxFarm then
            -- no car right now -> stand still and try again (never wander / wait in the road)
            notifyFn("Farm", "No car yet (" .. tostring(msgTrip) .. ") — standing still, trying again in 10s", "clock")
            Farm.status = "⏳ Waiting for your car (standing still)…"
            local release = Travel.holdStill()
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while S.BoxFarm and os.clock() - t0 < 10 do task.wait(0.25) end
            release()
        end
        task.wait(0.3)
        return okTrip
    end

    -- car to the gas station if far, then glide
    local function travelTo(pos, notifyFn)
        if needDrive() then driveToGas(notifyFn) end
        if not S.BoxFarm then return false end
        return Farm.glideTo(pos)
    end

    local function firePrompt(pr) firePP(pr) end

    Farm.firePrompt = firePrompt

    -- stand on the blue circle until the delivery completes
    local function deliver(notifyFn)
        local ring
        for _ = 1, 30 do ring = ringPart(); if ring then break end; task.wait(0.1) end
        if not ring then Farm.status = "❌ No blue circle found"; return false end
        local carriedKind = (function() local t = boxTool(); return t and KIND_OF_TOOL[t.Name] end)()
        local okD = (function()
        Farm.status = "Walking to the blue circle (box put away)…"
        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        -- put the box in the backpack while moving so it can't get stuck on shelves
        if hum then pcall(hum.UnequipTools, hum) end
        local standY = (hum and hum.HipHeight or 2) + 1.2
        local spot = ring.Position + Vector3.new(0, standY, 0)
        if not travelTo(spot, notifyFn) then equipBox(); return false end
        -- on the circle: take the box back out
        equipBox()
        for _ = 1, 10 do
            local _, held = boxTool()
            if held then break end
            task.wait(0.05); equipBox()
        end
        Farm.status = "On the circle — delivering…"
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local startT = os.clock()
        local since = os.clock()
        -- hold still on the ring until "complete" (the job needs ~5s)
        while S.BoxFarm and hrp and hrp.Parent and os.clock() - startT < 15 do
            if deliveryAction == "complete" and deliveryAt >= since then return true end
            if not isCarrying() and os.clock() - startT > 1 then return true end
            if deliveryAction == "cancel" and deliveryAt >= since then
                -- stepped off / moved: re-center and wait again
                since = os.clock()
            end
            local r = ringPart()
            if r then spot = r.Position + Vector3.new(0, standY, 0) end
            if (hrp.Position - spot).Magnitude > 1.5 then
                SESSION.tp(hrp, CFrame.new(spot) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation, "boxRing")
            end
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            equipBox()
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        return not isCarrying()
        end)()
        if okD and carriedKind then Farm.delivered[carriedKind] = (Farm.delivered[carriedKind] or 0) + 1 end
        return okD
    end

    function Farm.readyKind(kind)
        for _, st in ipairs(STATIONS) do if st.kind == kind then return ready(st) end end
        return false
    end

    local function pick()
        for _, st in ipairs(STATIONS) do
            local want
            if Farm.quota then
                want = (Farm.delivered[st.kind] or 0) < (Farm.quota[st.kind] or 0)
            else
                want = (st.kind == "green" and S.FarmGreen) or (st.kind == "gold" and S.FarmGold) or (st.kind == "normal" and S.FarmNormal)
            end
            if want and ready(st) then return st end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local running = false
    function Farm.start(notifyFn)
        if running then return end
        running = true
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        setNoclip(true)
        task.spawn(function()
            while S.BoxFarm do
                local ok, err = pcall(function()
                    local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then Farm.status = "Waiting to respawn"; task.wait(1); return end
                    -- far away -> spawn car, drive to the gas station, hop out
                    if needDrive() then driveToGas(notifyFn); return end
                    -- already holding a box -> deliver it first
                    if isCarrying() then
                        if deliver(notifyFn) then Farm.status = "✅ Delivered" end
                        task.wait(S.FarmDelay)
                        return
                    end
                    local st = pick()
                    if not st then Farm.status = "Waiting for boxes (timers)…"; task.wait(1); return end
                    local m = station(st)
                    local pr = stationPrompt(m)
                    local pos = stationPos(m)
                    if not (pr and pos) then banned[st.name] = os.clock() + 10; return end
                    Farm.status = "Going to " .. (st.kind == "green" and "🟢 green" or st.kind == "gold" and "🟡 gold" or "📦 normal") .. " box"
                    local hrp = L.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                    local dir = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X - pos.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z - pos.Z)
                    dir = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
                    local standAt = pos + dir * 3 + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
                    if not travelTo(standAt, notifyFn) then return end
                    Farm.status = "Picking up…"
                    firePrompt(pr)
                    for _ = 1, 20 do if isCarrying() then break end; task.wait(0.1) end
                    if not isCarrying() then
                        firePrompt(pr)
                        for _ = 1, 15 do if isCarrying() then break end; task.wait(0.1) end
                    end
                    if not isCarrying() then banned[st.name] = os.clock() + 10; Farm.status = "Couldn't pick up, trying another"; return end
                    if deliver(notifyFn) then Farm.status = "✅ Delivered" end
                    task.wait(S.FarmDelay)
                end)
                if not ok then Farm.status = "Error: " .. tostring(err); task.wait(1) end
            end
            if not S.MopFarm then setNoclip(false) end
            Farm.status = "Off"
            running = false
        end)
    end
    function Farm.isRunning() return running end
    function Farm.stop()
        S.BoxFarm = false
        if not S.MopFarm then setNoclip(false) end
    end

    function Farm.dump()
        local lines = { "==== BOX FARM INFO ====" }
        for _, st in ipairs(STATIONS) do
            local m = station(st)
            local pr = stationPrompt(m)
            lines[#lines+1] = string.format("%s: found=%s prompt=%s enabled=%s timer=%s", st.name, tostring(m ~= nil), tostring(pr ~= nil),
                tostring(pr and pr.Enabled), tostring(m and timerText(m)))
        end
        local r = ringPart()
        lines[#lines+1] = "ring: " .. (r and tostring(r.Position) or "none") .. "   carrying: " .. tostring(isCarrying())
        lines[#lines+1] = "last DeliveryState: " .. deliveryAction .. "   buff: " .. Farm.buff .. "   notify: " .. Farm.lastNotify
        lines[#lines+1] = "status: " .. Farm.status
        local txt = table.concat(lines, "\n")
        local cb = setclipboard or toclipboard or set_clipboard
        if cb then pcall(cb, txt) end
        return cb ~= nil
    end

    if S.BoxFarm then task.delay(4, function() if S.BoxFarm then Farm.start() end end) end
end

-- ================================================================
-- MOP FARM (exact - from your mop dump)
--   Job   : stand on the neon pad at the restaurant -> server sends
--           MopJobRemotes.ShowJobPrompt(true) -> we answer JobResponse("yes")
--           -> you get a "Mop" tool.
--   Clean : puddles are workspace.Puddle / workspace.BigPuddle (blue Glass
--           MeshParts).  Stand on one with the mop -> CleaningState
--           {action="start",duration=5} -> {action="complete",amount=..,multiplier=..}
--           Cleaned puddles go invisible (Transparency 1) until they come back.
--   Leave : walking away = CleaningState {action="cancel"} + JobNotify "You left the cleaning job."
--   Shop  : stand on the neon pad at the mop shop -> ShowShop(true) ->
--           BuyMop:FireServer("GoldenMop" | "EsmeraldMop") -> ShopFeedback(true/false, msg)
--           SyncOwned {GoldenMop=bool, EsmeraldMop=bool}
-- ================================================================
local Mop = {}
do
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local shopR = RS:FindFirstChild("MopShopRemotes")
    local jobR = RS:FindFirstChild("MopJobRemotes")
    local function remote(folder, name) return folder and folder:FindFirstChild(name) or nil end
    local rBuy  = remote(shopR, "BuyMop")
    local rSync = remote(shopR, "RequestSync")
    local rResp = remote(jobR, "JobResponse")

    local JOB_POS  = Vector3.new(-145.12, 364.24, 228.29)   -- neon pad (mop job, new spot after the update)
    local SHOP_POS = Vector3.new(-397.62, 364.95, 112.50)   -- neon pad (mop shop)
    local STAND_UP = 3.7                                      -- HRP height above a puddle
    Mop.JOB_POS, Mop.SHOP_POS = JOB_POS, SHOP_POS

    local MOPS = {
        EsmeraldMop = { label = "Emerald Mop", price = 1500, mult = 1.5 },
        GoldenMop   = { label = "Golden Mop",  price = 1000, mult = 1.3 },
        Mop         = { label = "Mop",         price = 0,    mult = 1 },
    }
    local PRIORITY = { "EsmeraldMop", "GoldenMop", "Mop" }
    Mop.MOPS, Mop.PRIORITY = MOPS, PRIORITY

    Mop.status, Mop.lastNotify, Mop.lastState, Mop.shopMsg = "Off", "", "", ""
    Mop.shopOk = nil
    Mop.owned = {}
    Mop.cleaned, Mop.earned = 0, 0
    local jobActive = false
    local promptOpen, promptAt = false, 0
    local shopOpen, shopOpenAt = false, 0
    local stateAction, stateAt = "", 0

    -- ── tools ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local function toolsByName()
        local t = {}
        for _, root in ipairs({ L.Character, L:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack") }) do
            if root then
                for _, c in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
                    if c:IsA("Tool") and MOPS[c.Name] then t[c.Name] = c end
                end
            end
        end
        return t
    end
    function Mop.bestTool()
        local t = toolsByName()
        for _, n in ipairs(PRIORITY) do if t[n] then return t[n], n end end
        return nil
    end
    function Mop.has(name) return Mop.owned[name] == true or toolsByName()[name] ~= nil end
    function Mop.bestOwned()
        for _, n in ipairs(PRIORITY) do if Mop.has(n) then return n end end
        return nil
    end
    local function equipMop()
        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local tool = Mop.bestTool()
        if hum and tool and tool.Parent ~= L.Character then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end) end
        return tool
    end

    -- ── game events ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    local function on(folder, name, f)
        local r = remote(folder, name)
        if r and r:IsA("RemoteEvent") then r.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...) pcall(f, ...) end) end
    end
    on(shopR, "SyncOwned", function(data)
        if type(data) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(data) do if MOPS[k] then Mop.owned[k] = v == true end end
        end
    end)
    on(shopR, "ShowShop", function(v) shopOpen = v == true; shopOpenAt = os.clock() end)
    on(shopR, "ShopFeedback", function(ok, msg)
        Mop.shopOk = ok
        Mop.shopMsg = type(msg) == "string" and msg or (type(ok) == "string" and ok or "")
        Mop.shopAt = os.clock()
    end)
    on(jobR, "ShowJobPrompt", function(v)
        promptOpen = v == true
        promptAt = os.clock()
        -- the game asks "take the job?" -> yes (after 0.7s, like a player tapping it)
        if promptOpen and S.MopFarm and rResp then
            task.delay(0.7, function() if not jobActive then Mop.sayYes() end end)
        end
    end)
    on(jobR, "JobNotify", function(a)
        local t = type(a) == "table" and tostring(a.text or a.message or "") or tostring(a)
        Mop.lastNotify = t
        local low = string.lower(t)
        if string.find(low, "left the") or string.find(low, "you left") or string.find(low, "job ended") or string.find(low, "quit") or string.find(low, "saliste") then jobActive = false end
    end)
    on(jobR, "CleaningState", function(a)
        if type(a) ~= "table" then return end
        stateAction, stateAt = tostring(a.action or ""), os.clock()
        if stateAction == "start" then jobActive = true; Mop.lastState = "cleaning…"
        elseif stateAction == "complete" then
            Mop.cleaned = Mop.cleaned + 1
            Mop.earned = Mop.earned + (tonumber(a.amount) or 0)
            Mop.lastState = "+$" .. tostring(a.amount or "?") .. " (x" .. tostring(a.multiplier or 1) .. ")"
        elseif stateAction == "cancel" then Mop.lastState = "cancelled" end
    end)
    L.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() jobActive = false end)
    local lastYes = 0
    function Mop.sayYes()
        if not rResp then return end
        lastYes = os.clock()
        pcall(function() rResp:FireServer("yes") end)
    end
    -- the job hands you a mop tool right after "yes" -> that's how we know we got it
    local function watchTools(root)
        if not root then return end
        root.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
            if c:IsA("Tool") and MOPS[c.Name] and os.clock() - lastYes < 5 then jobActive = true end
        end)
    end
    watchTools(L:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
    watchTools(L.Character)
    L.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c) if c:IsA("Backpack") then watchTools(c) end end)
    L.CharacterAdded:Connect(watchTools)
    if rSync then task.delay(2, function() pcall(function() rSync:FireServer() end) end) end

    -- ── puddles ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local puddles = {}
    local function isPuddle(d) return d:IsA("BasePart") and (d.Name == "Puddle" or d.Name == "BigPuddle") end
    local scanned = false
    function Mop.scan()
        if scanned then return end
        scanned = true
        task.spawn(function()
            for i, d in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if i % 2000 == 0 then task.wait() end
                if isPuddle(d) then puddles[#puddles+1] = d end
            end
        end)
        workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(d) if isPuddle(d) then puddles[#puddles+1] = d end end)
    end
    local skipUntil = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local function dirty(p) return p.Parent ~= nil and p.Transparency < 0.9 and p.Size.X > 2 end
    function Mop.puddleCount()
        local n, d = 0, 0
        for _, p in ipairs(puddles) do if p.Parent then n = n + 1; if dirty(p) then d = d + 1 end end end
        return d, n
    end
    -- another player is standing on / mopping this puddle -> skip it
    local function occupied(p)
        local r = math.max(p.Size.X, p.Size.Z) / 2 + 3
        for _, pl in ipairs(P:GetPlayers()) do
            if pl ~= L then
                local ch = pl.Character
                local h = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if h then
                    local d = h.Position - p.Position
                    local near = math.abs(d.Y) < 9 and Vector2.new(d.X, d.Z).Magnitude < r
                    if near then return true end
                    if (ch:GetAttribute("MopSweeping") or pl:GetAttribute("MopSweeping")) and Vector2.new(d.X, d.Z).Magnitude < r + 4 and math.abs(d.Y) < 12 then return true end
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    Mop.occupied = occupied
    local function nextPuddle()
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return nil end
        local best, bd = nil, math.huge
        for _, p in ipairs(puddles) do
            if dirty(p) and not (skipUntil[p] and skipUntil[p] > os.clock()) and (p.Position - JOB_POS).Magnitude < 250 and not occupied(p) then
                local d = (p.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bd then best, bd = p, d end
            end
        end
        return best
    end
    function Mop.jobSpot() return JOB_POS end

    -- ── moving: car if far (then hop out), glide the rest ────────
    local function goTo(pos, keep, notifyFn)
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return false end
        if S.FarmUseCar and Travel and (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude > S.FarmCarDist then
            Mop.status = "🚗 Driving (" .. ("above ground") .. ")…"
            for attempt = 1, 3 do
                local fin, okT, msgT = false, false, ""
                Travel.trip(pos, nil, notifyFn, true, function(o, m) okT, msgT, fin = o, m, true end)
                while not fin do
                    if not keep() then Travel.cancel() end
                    task.wait(0.25)
                end
                if string.find(tostring(msgT), "died", 1, true) then return false end   -- respawn -> the loop drives again
                if okT or not keep() or string.find(tostring(msgT), "Stop", 1, true) then break end
                notifyFn("Mop", "Car trip failed (" .. tostring(msgT) .. ") — trying the car again (" .. attempt .. "/3)", "car")
                task.wait(1.5)
                if attempt == 3 then
                    -- no car right now -> stand still and try again later (never wander / wait in the road)
                    Mop.status = "⏳ Waiting for your car (standing still)…"
                    local release = Travel.holdStill()
                    local t0 = os.clock()
                    while keep() and os.clock() - t0 < 10 do task.wait(0.25) end
                    release()
                    return false
                end
            end
            if not keep() then return false end
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
        return Farm.glideTo(pos, keep)
    end
    -- hold still on a spot for t seconds (or until done() is true)
    local function pinAt(pos)
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return function() end end
        local c = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not hrp.Parent then return end
            if (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude > 0.5 then SESSION.tp(hrp, CFrame.new(pos) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation, "pinAt") end
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
        return function() c:Disconnect() end
    end
    local function holdAt(pos, t, keep, done)
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return false end
        local unpin = pinAt(pos)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        local res = false
        while hrp.Parent and keep() and os.clock() - t0 < t do
            if done and done() then res = true; break end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        unpin()
        if not res and done then res = done() and true or false end
        return res
    end

    -- ── the job pad itself (the neon floor part the game listens to) ──
    local function findJobPad()
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp and (hrp.Position - JOB_POS).Magnitude > 120 then
            pcall(function() L:RequestStreamAroundAsync(JOB_POS, 3) end)
        end
        local best, bestScore
        local ok, parts = pcall(function() return workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(JOB_POS, 16) end)
        if not ok or not parts then return nil end
        for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") and not (L.Character and p:IsDescendantOf(L.Character)) and p.Size.Y <= 4
                and p.Size.X >= 2 and p.Size.Z >= 2 and p.Size.X <= 30 and p.Size.Z <= 30 then
                local n = string.lower(p.Name)
                local named = n:find("job") or n:find("pad") or n:find("trigger") or n:find("zone") or n:find("touch") or n:find("prompt")
                if p.Material == Enum.Material.Neon or named then
                    local d = Vector3.new(p.Position.X - JOB_POS.X, 0, p.Position.Z - JOB_POS.Z).Magnitude
                    local score = d - (named and 6 or 0) - (p.Material == Enum.Material.Neon and 3 or 0)
                    if not bestScore or score < bestScore then best, bestScore = p, score end
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end
    -- stand on the pad for real: walk in small steps on it (real movement = the game
    -- registers the touch) + tell the game every part of you is touching it
    local function standOnPad(pad, standPos, t, keep, done)
        local char = L.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then return false end
        local t0, lastTouch, step = os.clock(), 0, 0
        local wig = { Vector3.new(1.2, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, 1.2), Vector3.new(-1.2, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, -1.2) }
        while hrp.Parent and keep() and os.clock() - t0 < t do
            if done and done() then return true end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - lastTouch > 0.6 then
                lastTouch = now
                step = step % #wig + 1
                pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(standPos + wig[step]) end)
                if pad and pad.Parent and firetouchinterest then
                    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            pcall(firetouchinterest, part, pad, 0)
                            task.delay(0.15, function() pcall(firetouchinterest, part, pad, 1) end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            -- drifted off the pad -> walk back on (never teleport: teleports don't count as touching)
            if Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X - standPos.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z - standPos.Z).Magnitude > 4 then
                pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(standPos) end)
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        return done and done() or false
    end

    -- ── take the job ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    local function takeJob(notifyFn)
        local keep = function() return S.MopFarm end
        local sides = { Vector3.new(0, 0, 9), Vector3.new(9, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, -9) }
        for attempt = 1, 3 do
            if not keep() then return false end
            Mop.status = "Going to the mop job…" .. (attempt > 1 and (" (try " .. attempt .. ")") or "")
            local padPart = findJobPad()
            local pad = padPart and Vector3.new(padPart.Position.X, padPart.Position.Y + padPart.Size.Y / 2 + 3, padPart.Position.Z)
                or (JOB_POS + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0))
            -- stop next to the pad, then WALK onto it so the game notices you
            if not goTo(pad + sides[attempt], keep, notifyFn) then return false end
            padPart = padPart or findJobPad()
            if padPart then pad = Vector3.new(padPart.Position.X, padPart.Position.Y + padPart.Size.Y / 2 + 3, padPart.Position.Z) end
            holdAt(pad + sides[attempt], 0.4, keep)
            local since = os.clock()
            Farm.glideTo(pad, keep)
            Mop.status = "Taking the mop job…"
            standOnPad(padPart, pad, 6, keep, function() return jobActive or promptAt > since end)
            if not jobActive and promptAt > since then
                -- prompt showed -> the auto "yes" goes out at 0.7s; give it time, then say it again
                standOnPad(padPart, pad, 2, keep, function() return jobActive end)
                if not jobActive then Mop.sayYes(); standOnPad(padPart, pad, 2.5, keep, function() return jobActive end) end
            end
            if jobActive then
                for _ = 1, 15 do if Mop.bestTool() then break end; task.wait(0.1) end
                Mop.status = "✅ Got the job"
                return true
            end
        end
        -- no prompt at all but you have a mop -> maybe the job is already running; try cleaning
        if Mop.bestTool() then jobActive = true; return true end
        return false
    end

    -- ── clean one puddle ─────────────────────────────────────────
    local function clean(p)
        local keep = function() return S.MopFarm end
        local spot = p.Position + Vector3.new(0, STAND_UP, 0)
        Mop.status = "🧽 Going to a puddle…"
        if occupied(p) then return "taken" end
        if not Farm.glideTo(spot, function() return S.MopFarm and not occupied(p) end) then return occupied(p) and "taken" or "fail" end
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return "fail" end
        equipMop()
        local unpin = pinAt(spot)
        local since = os.clock()
        local startedAt, cancels, result = nil, 0, nil
        local lastEquip = 0
        Mop.status = "🧽 Mopping…"
        while keep() and hrp.Parent do
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - lastEquip > 0.5 then equipMop(); lastEquip = now end
            if stateAt > since then
                if stateAction == "start" and not startedAt then
                    startedAt = stateAt
                elseif stateAction == "complete" then
                    result = "ok"; break
                elseif stateAction == "cancel" and startedAt then
                    -- got cancelled -> stay on it, it starts again
                    cancels = cancels + 1
                    startedAt = nil
                    since = now
                    if cancels >= 3 then result = "fail"; break end
                end
            end
            if startedAt then
                Mop.status = string.format("🧽 Mopping… %.0fs", math.max(0, 5 - (now - startedAt)))
                -- puddle is gone and the 5s are up (missed the event) -> done
                if not dirty(p) and now - startedAt > 5.2 then result = "ok"; break end
                if now - startedAt > 12 then result = "fail"; break end
            elseif occupied(p) then
                result = "taken"; break                     -- someone else is mopping it
            elseif now - since > 5 then
                result = occupied(p) and "taken" or "nostart"; break
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        -- never leave before the full 5 seconds
        if result == "ok" and startedAt then
            while keep() and os.clock() - startedAt < 5.3 do task.wait(0.1) end
        end
        unpin()
        return result or "fail"
    end

    -- ── buy a mop ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local buying = false
    function Mop.isBuying() return buying end
    function Mop.buy(name, notifyFn)
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        name = (name == "GoldenMop") and "GoldenMop" or "EsmeraldMop"
        if buying then return false end
        if not rBuy then notifyFn("Mop", "BuyMop not found in this game", "x"); return false end
        if Mop.has(name) then notifyFn("Mop", "You already have the " .. MOPS[name].label, "check"); return true end
        buying = true
        local keep = function() return buying end
        local wasNoclip = S.BoxFarm or S.MopFarm
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            Farm.setNoclip(true)
            Mop.status = "Going to the mop shop…"
            local pad = SHOP_POS + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
            goTo(pad, keep, notifyFn)
            -- stand on the pad until the shop opens for you
            local since = os.clock()
            if not (shopOpen and os.clock() - shopOpenAt < 30) then
                holdAt(pad, 4, keep, function() return shopOpen and shopOpenAt > since end)
                if not shopOpen then
                    holdAt(pad + Vector3.new(0, 0, 8), 0.6, keep)
                    holdAt(pad, 4, keep, function() return shopOpen end)
                end
            end
            Mop.status = "Buying the " .. MOPS[name].label .. "…"
            local before = Mop.shopAt or 0
            rBuy:FireServer(name)
            holdAt(pad, 4, keep, function() return (Mop.shopAt or 0) > before or toolsByName()[name] ~= nil end)
            -- close the shop window
            pcall(function()
                local g = pg:FindFirstChild("MopShopGui")
                local f = g and g:FindFirstChild("MopShopFrame")
                if f then f.Visible = false end
            end)
        end)
        if not wasNoclip then Farm.setNoclip(false) end
        buying = false
        local got = Mop.has(name)
        local fresh = Mop.shopAt and os.clock() - Mop.shopAt < 10
        if got then notifyFn("Mop", fresh and Mop.shopMsg ~= "" and Mop.shopMsg or ("Got the " .. MOPS[name].label .. "!"), "check")
        elseif fresh and Mop.shopMsg ~= "" then notifyFn("Mop", "Shop: " .. Mop.shopMsg, "x")
        elseif not ok then notifyFn("Mop", "Buy error: " .. tostring(err), "x")
        elseif not shopOpen then notifyFn("Mop", "The shop didn't open — stand on the shop pad and try again", "x")
        else notifyFn("Mop", "No answer from the shop", "x") end
        Mop.status = S.MopFarm and "Back to work" or "Off"
        return got
    end

    -- ── main loop ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local running = false
    local triedBuy = {}
    function Mop.start(notifyFn)
        if running then return end
        running = true
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        Mop.scan()
        Farm.setNoclip(true)
        task.spawn(function()
            local noStarts = 0
            while S.MopFarm do
                local ok, err = pcall(function()
                    local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then Mop.status = "Waiting to respawn"; task.wait(1); return end
                    -- 1) buy the better mop first (only if Auto Buy is on)
                    if S.MopAutoBuy and not S.BothFarm and not Mop.has(S.MopBuy) and not triedBuy[S.MopBuy] then
                        triedBuy[S.MopBuy] = true
                        Mop.buy(S.MopBuy, notifyFn)
                        return
                    end
                    -- 2) no job (or no mop) -> take the job
                    if not jobActive or not Mop.bestTool() then
                        if not takeJob(notifyFn) then Mop.status = "Couldn't get the job, retrying…"; task.wait(1) end
                        return
                    end
                    -- 3) clean the nearest dirty puddle
                    local p = nextPuddle()
                    if not p then
                        Mop.status = "All clean — waiting for puddles…"
                        local hrp = L.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                        if (hrp.Position - JOB_POS).Magnitude > 200 then Farm.glideTo(JOB_POS + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0), function() return S.MopFarm end) end
                        task.wait(1)
                        return
                    end
                    local r = clean(p)
                    if r == "ok" then
                        noStarts = 0
                        Mop.status = "✅ Cleaned " .. Mop.cleaned .. " · $" .. Mop.earned
                        task.wait(math.max(S.FarmDelay, 1))
                    elseif r == "taken" then
                        skipUntil[p] = os.clock() + 8
                        Mop.status = "Another player is on that puddle — next one"
                    elseif r == "nostart" then
                        skipUntil[p] = os.clock() + 15
                        noStarts = noStarts + 1
                        -- 3 free puddles in a row didn't start -> the job isn't active anymore
                        if noStarts >= 3 then jobActive = false; noStarts = 0 end
                    else
                        skipUntil[p] = os.clock() + 10
                    end
                end)
                if not ok then Mop.status = "Error: " .. tostring(err); task.wait(1) end
            end
            if not S.BoxFarm then Farm.setNoclip(false) end
            Mop.status = "Off"
            running = false
        end)
    end
    function Mop.isRunning() return running end
    function Mop.stop()
        S.MopFarm = false
        buying = false
        if not S.BoxFarm then Farm.setNoclip(false) end
    end

    function Mop.dump()
        local d, n = Mop.puddleCount()
        local own = {}
        for _, k in ipairs(PRIORITY) do if Mop.has(k) then own[#own+1] = k end end
        local lines = {
            "==== MOP FARM INFO ====",
            "remotes: shop=" .. tostring(shopR ~= nil) .. " job=" .. tostring(jobR ~= nil),
            "owned: " .. (#own > 0 and table.concat(own, ", ") or "none") .. "   tool: " .. tostring((select(2, Mop.bestTool()))),
            "job active: " .. tostring(jobActive) .. "   prompt: " .. tostring(promptOpen) .. "   shop open: " .. tostring(shopOpen),
            "puddles: " .. d .. " dirty / " .. n .. " total",
            "last cleaning: " .. stateAction .. " " .. Mop.lastState .. "   notify: " .. Mop.lastNotify,
            "shop: " .. tostring(Mop.shopOk) .. " " .. Mop.shopMsg,
            "cleaned " .. Mop.cleaned .. "  earned $" .. Mop.earned,
            "status: " .. Mop.status,
        }
        local txt = table.concat(lines, "\n")
        local cb = setclipboard or toclipboard or set_clipboard
        if cb then pcall(cb, txt) end
        return cb ~= nil
    end

    if S.MopFarm then task.delay(4, function() if S.MopFarm then Mop.start() end end) end
end

-- ================================================================
-- BOTH JOBS
--   car -> boxes (2 green + 5 gold) -> mop shop (buys the best mop you can
--   afford, optional) -> restaurant job -> mops until green/gold boxes are
--   back in stock -> drives back -> repeat
-- ================================================================
local Both = { status = "Off", phase = "", cycles = 0 }
do
    local running = false
    local function waitUntil(cond, maxT)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while S.BothFarm and not cond() and os.clock() - t0 < (maxT or 20) do task.wait(0.25) end
    end
    local function quotaDone()
        return (Farm.delivered.green or 0) >= S.BothGreen and (Farm.delivered.gold or 0) >= S.BothGold
    end
    -- green/gold that we still want AND are in stock
    local function wantedInStock()
        return ((Farm.delivered.green or 0) < S.BothGreen and Farm.readyKind("green"))
            or ((Farm.delivered.gold or 0) < S.BothGold and Farm.readyKind("gold"))
    end
    local function anyInStock()
        return (S.BothGreen > 0 and Farm.readyKind("green")) or (S.BothGold > 0 and Farm.readyKind("gold"))
    end
    local function cash()
        local st = L:FindFirstChild("Stats")
        local c = st and st:FindFirstChild("Cash")
        return c and tonumber(c.Value) or 0
    end

    local function boxPhase(notifyFn)
        Both.phase = "📦 Boxes"
        Farm.delivered = { green = 0, gold = 0, normal = 0 }
        Farm.quota = { green = S.BothGreen, gold = S.BothGold, normal = 0 }
        S.BoxFarm = true
        Farm.start(notifyFn)
        local emptySince
        while S.BothFarm do
            local carrying = Farm.isCarrying()
            if quotaDone() and not carrying then break end
            if not carrying and not wantedInStock() then
                emptySince = emptySince or os.clock()
                if os.clock() - emptySince > 5 then break end      -- out of stock
            else
                emptySince = nil
            end
            Both.status = string.format("📦 Boxes  🟢 %d/%d  🟡 %d/%d\n%s", Farm.delivered.green, S.BothGreen, Farm.delivered.gold, S.BothGold, Farm.status)
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
        if not S.BothFarm then return end
        S.BoxFarm = false
        Farm.quota = nil
        waitUntil(function() return not Farm.isRunning() end, 15)
        notifyFn("Both Jobs", string.format("Boxes done (🟢 %d  🟡 %d) — going to mop", Farm.delivered.green, Farm.delivered.gold), "check")
    end

    local function buyPhase(notifyFn)
        if not S.BothBuyMop then return end
        local money = cash()
        for _, name in ipairs({ "EsmeraldMop", "GoldenMop" }) do
            if Mop.has(name) then return end                        -- already have this one (or better)
            local price = Mop.MOPS[name].price
            if money >= price then
                Both.phase = "🛒 Mop Shop"
                Both.status = "🛒 Buying the " .. Mop.MOPS[name].label .. " ($" .. price .. ")…"
                Mop.buy(name, notifyFn)
                return
            end
        end
        -- can't afford anything better -> skip
    end

    local function mopPhase(notifyFn)
        Both.phase = "🧽 Mop"
        S.MopFarm = true
        Mop.start(notifyFn)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while S.BothFarm do
            local c = Farm.counts()
            local canSee = (c.green and c.green.t > 0) or (c.gold and c.gold.t > 0)
            if anyInStock() then break end
            -- box stations too far to see -> go check every 5 min
            if not canSee and os.clock() - t0 > 300 then break end
            local function line(e) return (not e or e.t == 0) and "?" or e.r > 0 and "✅" or (e.timer or "⏳") end
            Both.status = "🧽 Mopping until boxes restock  (🟢 " .. line(c.green) .. "  🟡 " .. line(c.gold) .. ")\n" .. Mop.status
            task.wait(1)
        end
        if not S.BothFarm then return end
        -- let the current puddle finish
        waitUntil(function() return not string.find(Mop.status, "Mopping") end, 8)
        S.MopFarm = false
        waitUntil(function() return not Mop.isRunning() end, 15)
        notifyFn("Both Jobs", "Boxes are back in stock — driving there", "car")
    end

    function Both.start(notifyFn)
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        if running then return end
        running = true
        S.BothFarm = true
        Farm.setNoclip(true)
        task.spawn(function()
            -- let any old box/mop loop finish first
            waitUntil(function() return not Farm.isRunning() and not Mop.isRunning() end, 15)
            while S.BothFarm do
                local ok, err = pcall(function()
                    local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then Both.status = "Waiting to respawn"; task.wait(1); return end
                    if anyInStock() or Farm.isCarrying() then boxPhase(notifyFn) end
                    if not S.BothFarm then return end
                    buyPhase(notifyFn)
                    if not S.BothFarm then return end
                    mopPhase(notifyFn)
                    Both.cycles = Both.cycles + 1
                end)
                if not ok then Both.status = "Error: " .. tostring(err); task.wait(1) end
            end
            running = false
        end)
    end
    function Both.stop()
        S.BothFarm = false
        Farm.quota = nil
        S.BoxFarm, S.MopFarm = false, false
        Farm.stop(); Mop.stop()
        pcall(function() if Travel.isBusy() then Travel.cancel() end end)
        Both.status, Both.phase = "Off", ""
    end
    function Both.isRunning() return running end

    if S.BothFarm then task.delay(4, function() if S.BothFarm then Both.start() end end) end
end

-- ================================================================
-- ANTI AFK  (always on - you won't get kicked for idling)
-- ================================================================
do
    local VU = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
    pcall(function()
        if getconnections then
            for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(L.Idled)) do pcall(function() c:Disable() end) end
        end
    end)
    L.Idled:Connect(function()
        pcall(function()
            VU:CaptureController()
            VU:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
        end)
    end)
end

-- ================================================================
-- AUTO FISHING  (new game update — built from your fishing dump)
--   Shop pad  : BuyFish  -> ShowShop(true) -> FishingRemotes.BuyRod:FireServer(rod / bait name)
--               rods are "for this life" (you lose them when you die)
--   Cast      : equip rod at the water's edge -> Cast:FireServer(power 0..1)
--               power = distance (15 .. 150 studs) in the direction you face
--   Bite      : FishingState("Bite", {token,start,width,speed,startedAt,hits,...})
--               marker = ping-pong of (serverTime - startedAt) * speed  (0..1)
--               marker inside [start, start+width] -> Reel:FireServer(token, serverTime, hits+1)
--   Caught    : FishingState("Caught", {name,value,...}) -> fish goes to your bag (18 max)
--   Sell pad  : SellFish -> ShowSell(true) -> Sell all
-- ================================================================
local Fish = {}
do
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local fr = RS:FindFirstChild("FishingRemotes")
    local function rem(n) return fr and fr:FindFirstChild(n) or nil end
    local rCast, rReel, rBuy, rSell = rem("Cast"), rem("Reel"), rem("BuyRod"), rem("Sell")
    Fish.available = (rCast ~= nil and rReel ~= nil)

    local SHOP_POS = Vector3.new(-1432.3, 393.7, 398.4)    -- BuyFish pad
    local SELL_POS = Vector3.new(-1679.0, 393.7, 334.0)    -- SellFish pad
    local SPOT     = Vector3.new(-1230.06, 351.93, 394.39)  -- YOUR spot (down by the water, zone 1)
    local SPOT_DIR = Vector3.new(-229.8, 0, -13.6).Unit     -- face out over the water (toward the middle of zone 1)
    local CAPACITY = 18
    local NEAR_DIST = 450                                   -- farther than this = car from the city
    local DOCK_CAR_DIST = 130                               -- on the dock: farther than this = car (sell pad <-> shop)
    Fish.SHOP_POS, Fish.SELL_POS = SHOP_POS, SELL_POS

    Fish.RODS = {
        { name = "FishingRodBasic",   label = "Basic Rod ($75)" },
        { name = "FishingRodGolden",  label = "Golden Rod ($300 · luck x1.35)" },
        { name = "FishingRodDiamond", label = "Diamond Rod ($750 · luck x1.8)" },
    }
    Fish.BAITS = {
        { name = "FishFoodCommon",    label = "Common Food ($25 · luck x1.7)" },
        { name = "FishFoodRare",      label = "Rare Food ($50 · luck x2.35)" },
        { name = "FishFoodSuperRare", label = "Super Rare Food ($100 · luck x3.75)" },
    }

    if (tonumber(S.FishSpotV) or 0) < 3 then S.FishSpot = ""; S.FishSpotV = 3 end   -- pier fishing now (old rock spot dropped)
    do  -- level 1 = the pier: an old saved level-1 spot down by the rock (below the pier) is dropped
        local y = tonumber(string.match(tostring(S.FishSpot1 or ""), "^[%-%d%.]+,([%-%d%.]+),"))
        if y and y < 385 then S.FishSpot1 = "" end
    end
    S.FishUnder = false                                    -- underground doesn't work anymore
    do  -- bait is required now: old configs had "None"
        local okB = false
        for _, b in ipairs(Fish.BAITS) do if b.name == S.FishBait then okB = true end end
        if not okB then S.FishBait = "FishFoodCommon" end
    end
    Fish.status, Fish.lastNotify, Fish.lastFish = "Off", "", ""
    -- small log of what Auto Fish did (in Copy Fishing Info) so bugs are easy to find
    local TRACE, T0 = {}, os.clock()
    local function trace(msg)
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local p = hrp and hrp.Position
        TRACE[#TRACE + 1] = string.format("[%6.1f] %s%s", os.clock() - T0, tostring(msg), p and string.format("  @(%.0f, %.0f, %.0f)", p.X, p.Y, p.Z) or "")
        if #TRACE > 40 then table.remove(TRACE, 1) end
    end
    Fish.trace = trace
    Fish.caught, Fish.earned, Fish.sold, Fish.misses = 0, 0, 0, 0
    Fish.bag, Fish.baits, Fish.owned = {}, {}, {}
    local shopOpen, shopAt, sellOpen, sellAt = false, 0, false, 0
    local fbAt, fbOk, fbMsg = 0, nil, ""
    local mine = false   -- true while WE fire Sell (the learn hook ignores our own guesses)
    local notifyAt, invAt = 0, 0
    -- current catch
    local st = { name = "", at = 0, token = nil, start = 0, width = 0, speed = 0.5, startedAt = 0, hits = 0, round = 0 }

    local function cash()
        local s = L:FindFirstChild("Stats")
        local c = s and s:FindFirstChild("Cash")
        return c and tonumber(c.Value) or 0
    end
    function Fish.bagCount()
        local n = 0
        for _, v in pairs(Fish.bag) do n = n + (tonumber(v) or 0) end
        return n
    end
    function Fish.baitCount(name)
        if not name or name == "None" then return 0 end
        return tonumber(Fish.baits[name]) or 0
    end
    -- all bait you have (the game uses your best one automatically)
    function Fish.baitTotal()
        local n = 0
        for _, v in pairs(Fish.baits) do n = n + (tonumber(v) or 0) end
        return n
    end

    -- ── listen to the game ───────────────────────────────────────
    local function on(n, fn)
        local r = rem(n)
        if r and r:IsA("RemoteEvent") then r.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...) if ALIVE() then pcall(fn, ...) end end) end
    end
    on("ShowShop", function(v) shopOpen = (v == true); if shopOpen then shopAt = os.clock() end end)
    on("ShowSell", function(v) sellOpen = (v == true); if sellOpen then sellAt = os.clock() end end)
    on("SyncInventory", function(fish, baits)
        if type(fish) == "table" then Fish.bag = fish end
