        if type(baits) == "table" then Fish.baits = baits end
        invAt = os.clock()
    end)
    on("SyncOwned", function(owned, baits)
        if type(owned) == "table" then Fish.owned = owned end
        if type(baits) == "table" then Fish.baits = baits end
    end)
    on("ShopFeedback", function(ok, msg) fbAt, fbOk, fbMsg = os.clock(), ok == true, tostring(msg or "") end)
    on("Notify", function(msg, kind) Fish.lastNotify = tostring(msg or ""); notifyAt = os.clock() end)
    on("FishingState", function(kind, d)
        d = type(d) == "table" and d or {}
        if kind == "GuideZones" then
            for _, z in pairs(type(d.zones) == "table" and d.zones or {}) do
                if type(z) == "table" and tonumber(z.number) then Fish.unlocked[tonumber(z.number)] = z.unlocked == true end
            end
            return
        end
        st.name, st.at = tostring(kind), os.clock()
        if d.token then st.token = d.token end
        if d.start then st.start = tonumber(d.start) or st.start end
        if d.width then st.width = tonumber(d.width) or st.width end
        if d.speed then st.speed = tonumber(d.speed) or st.speed end
        if d.startedAt then st.startedAt = tonumber(d.startedAt) or st.startedAt end
        if d.hits then st.hits = tonumber(d.hits) or st.hits end
        if d.round then st.round = tonumber(d.round) or st.round end
        if kind == "Caught" then
            Fish.caught = Fish.caught + 1
            Fish.earned = Fish.earned + (tonumber(d.value) or 0)
            Fish.lastFish = tostring(d.name or "?") .. (d.rarity and (" (" .. tostring(d.rarity) .. ")") or "")
        elseif kind == "Miss" then
            Fish.misses = Fish.misses + 1
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        for _, en in ipairs({ "LevelProgressEvent", "XPProgressEvent" }) do
            local ev = RS:FindFirstChild(en)
            if ev and ev:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                ev.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(d) if type(d) == "table" and tonumber(d.level) then Fish.level = tonumber(d.level) end end)
            end
        end
    end)
    -- learn the real "Sell" args the first time the game's sell button sends them
    pcall(function()
        if rSell then
            learnHook({ rSell }, function(_, ...)
                if mine or getnamecallmethod() ~= "FireServer" then return end
                local args = { ... }
                pcall(function() S.FishSellArgs = Http:JSONEncode(args) end)
            end)
        end
    end)

    -- ── tools ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function Fish.rodTool()
        local char = L.Character
        local bp = L:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        for _, root in ipairs({ char, bp }) do
            if root then
                for _, c in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
                    if c:IsA("Tool") and string.sub(c.Name, 1, 10) == "FishingRod" then return c, root == char end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil, false
    end
    local function equipRod()
        local t, held = Fish.rodTool()
        if not t then return false end
        if held then return true end
        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return false end
        pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t) end)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < 2 do
            if t.Parent == L.Character then task.wait(0.5); return true end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        return t.Parent == L.Character
    end

    -- ── moving ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local function hrpOf() return L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
    local goToPlain
    -- recorded ramp route: S.FishRoute = "x,y,z;x,y,z;..." (bottom of the ramp -> top of the dock)
    -- built-in ramp (from you): start at the bottom on the road -> end at the top by the fishing shop
    local RAMP_START = Vector3.new(-878.02, 363.81, 334.02)
    local RAMP_END   = Vector3.new(-1460.78, 392.92, 333.03)
    function Fish.route()
        local pts = {}
        for x, y, z in string.gmatch(tostring(S.FishRoute or ""), "([%-%d%.]+),([%-%d%.]+),([%-%d%.]+)") do
            pts[#pts + 1] = Vector3.new(tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z))
        end
        if #pts < 2 then return { RAMP_START, RAMP_END }, false end
        return pts, true
    end
    local recConn, recPts = nil, nil
    function Fish.isRecording() return recConn ~= nil end
    local recKey = "FishRoute"
    function Fish.recStart(key)
        if recConn then return end
        recKey = key or "FishRoute"
        local spacing = (recKey == "FishSellRoute" and 5 or 10)
        recPts = {}
        local last = nil
        recConn = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local hrp = hrpOf()
            if not hrp then return end
            local car = Travel and Travel.myCar and Travel.myCar()
            local hum = L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local pos = hrp.Position
            if car and hum and hum.SeatPart then
                local ok, cf = pcall(car.GetPivot, car)
                if ok then pos = cf.Position end
            end
            if not last or (pos - last).Magnitude >= spacing then
                recPts[#recPts + 1] = pos
                last = pos
            end
        end)
    end
    function Fish.recStop()
        if recConn then recConn:Disconnect(); recConn = nil end
        local pts = recPts or {}
        recPts = nil
        -- add the exact end point
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if hrp and #pts > 0 and (hrp.Position - pts[#pts]).Magnitude > 3 then
            local car = Travel and Travel.myCar and Travel.myCar()
            local hum = L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local pos = hrp.Position
            if car and hum and hum.SeatPart then pcall(function() pos = car:GetPivot().Position end) end
            pts[#pts + 1] = pos
        end
        if #pts < 2 then return false, #pts end
        local t = {}
        for _, v in ipairs(pts) do t[#t + 1] = string.format("%.1f,%.1f,%.1f", v.X, v.Y, v.Z) end
        S[recKey] = table.concat(t, ";")
        return true, #pts
    end
    function Fish.clearRoute(key) S[key or "FishRoute"] = "" end
    function Fish.recKey() return recKey end

    local holdRelease, releaseHold, holdAt = nil, nil, nil   -- (set further down, next to pin)
    -- ── the dock: EVERYTHING goes through the end of the ramp ─────
    --   (walking straight across made it walk off the edge of the ramp / dock)
    local function flatD(a, b) return Vector3.new(a.X - b.X, 0, a.Z - b.Z).Magnitude end
    local function rampTop() local r = Fish.route(); return r[#r] end
    local function isUp(p) local r = Fish.route(); return p.Y > (r[1].Y + r[#r].Y) / 2 end
    -- on foot on the dock: ramp end first, then the place
    local function dockWalk(pos, keep)
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return false end
        local top = rampTop()
        if flatD(hrp.Position, top) > 10 and flatD(pos, top) > 10 and flatD(hrp.Position, pos) > 15 then
            Fish.status = "🏃 Going to the end of the ramp…"
            Farm.glideTo(top + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), keep)
        end
        if not keep() then return false end
        return Farm.glideTo(pos, keep)
    end
    -- stuck somewhere on the ramp on foot -> walk ALONG the ramp (never straight off the side)
    local function walkRamp(route, up, keep)
        local pts = {}
        if up then for k = 1, #route do pts[#pts + 1] = route[k] end
        else for k = #route, 1, -1 do pts[#pts + 1] = route[k] end end
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return false end
        local i = 1
        while i < #pts and flatD(pts[i + 1], hrp.Position) < flatD(pts[i + 1], pts[i]) do i = i + 1 end
        Fish.status = up and "🏃 Running up the ramp…" or "🏃 Running down the ramp…"
        for k = i, #pts do
            if not keep() then return false end
            Farm.glideTo(pts[k] + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), keep)
        end
        return true
    end
    -- at the water's edge the car would spawn off the dock into the water ->
    -- run to where the ramp ends first, the car comes up there
    local function carSpot(keep)
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return end
        local top = rampTop()
        if hrp.Position.Y < Fish.spot().Y - 2 and Fish.surface then Fish.surface(keep) end
        hrp = hrpOf()
        if hrp and isUp(hrp.Position) and flatD(hrp.Position, top) > 12
            and (flatD(hrp.Position, Fish.spot()) < 45 or flatD(hrp.Position, top) <= DOCK_CAR_DIST) then
            Fish.status = "🏃 Going to the end of the ramp for the car…"
            trace("walk to the ramp end for the car")
            Farm.glideTo(top + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), keep)
        end
    end
    local function carTrip(target, keep, notifyFn, hopOut, opts)
        for attempt = 1, 3 do
            local fin, okT, msgT = false, false, ""
            Travel.trip(target, nil, notifyFn, hopOut, function(o, m) okT, msgT, fin = o, m, true end, opts)
            while not fin do
                if not keep() then Travel.cancel() end
                task.wait(0.25)
            end
            trace("car trip -> " .. string.format("(%.0f, %.0f, %.0f)", target.X, target.Y, target.Z) .. " : " .. (okT and "ok" or ("FAIL " .. tostring(msgT))))
            if okT then return true, "" end
            if string.find(tostring(msgT), "died", 1, true) or not keep() or string.find(tostring(msgT), "Stop", 1, true) then return false, msgT end
            notifyFn("Fishing", "Car trip failed (" .. tostring(msgT) .. ") — trying again (" .. attempt .. "/3)", "car")
            task.wait(1.5)
        end
        return false, "car trip failed"
    end
    -- get in your car right where you are (spawns it / brings it / runs to it)
    local function boardHere(keep, notifyFn)
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return false end
        local car = Travel.myCar and Travel.myCar()
        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if car and hum and hum.SeatPart and hum.SeatPart:IsDescendantOf(car) then return true end
        return carTrip(hrp.Position, keep, notifyFn, false, { above = true, noDrive = true })
    end
    -- drive fixed points at 70; if you fell out of the car, get back in and keep going
    local function drivePts(pts, keep, notifyFn, label, opts)
        local okP, msg = false, "no car"
        for attempt = 1, 3 do
            if not keep() then return false, "stopped" end
            if not boardHere(keep, notifyFn) then msg = "couldn't get in the car"; break end
            okP, msg = Travel.rampDrive(pts, notifyFn, opts)
            trace(label .. ": " .. (okP and "ok" or ("FAIL " .. tostring(msg))))
            if okP or tostring(msg) ~= "not in the car" then break end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
        return okP, msg
    end
    -- up the ramp: car -> bottom of the ramp -> drive the ramp at 70 -> hop out at the ramp end -> walk
    local function goUp(pos, route, keep, notifyFn)
        Fish.status = "🚗 Driving to the ramp…"
        local ok = carTrip(route[1], keep, notifyFn, false)
        if not keep() then return false end
        local okP, msg = false, "no car"
        if ok then
            Fish.status = "🚗 Driving up the ramp…"
            if Fish.routeToPark then route = Fish.routeToPark() end     -- 🔒 stop at the park spot (before the ramp end)
            okP, msg = drivePts(route, keep, notifyFn, "ramp up")
            -- far along the dock (sell pad) -> keep driving from the ramp end, straight there
            local top = route[#route]
            if okP and keep() and flatD(top, pos) > DOCK_CAR_DIST then
                local f = Vector3.new(pos.X - top.X, 0, pos.Z - top.Z)
                Fish.status = "🚗 Driving…"
                drivePts({ pos - (f.Magnitude > 1 and f.Unit * 6 or Vector3.zero) }, keep, notifyFn, "ramp end -> place")
            end
        end
        Travel.hopOut()
        if not keep() then return false end
        if not okP then
            notifyFn("Fishing", "Ramp drive stopped (" .. tostring(msg) .. ") — running up the ramp", "footprints")
            walkRamp(route, true, keep)
        end
        return dockWalk(pos, keep)
    end
    -- down the ramp: ramp end -> car -> drive the ramp down at 70 -> drive to the place
    local function goDown(pos, route, keep, notifyFn)
        carSpot(keep)
        Fish.status = "🚗 Driving down the ramp…"
        local rev = {}
        for i = #route, 1, -1 do rev[#rev + 1] = route[i] end
        local okP, msgD = drivePts(rev, keep, notifyFn, "ramp down")
        if not keep() then Travel.hopOut(); return false end
        if not okP then
            Travel.hopOut()
            walkRamp(route, false, keep)
        end
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if hrp and (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude > 20 then
            Fish.status = "🚗 Driving…"
            carTrip(pos, keep, notifyFn, true)      -- stops on solid ground next to it, gets you out, walks the last bit
        end
        Travel.hopOut()
        Fish.status = "🚶 Walking to the spot…"
        return Farm.glideTo(pos, keep)
    end
    local function goTo(pos, keep, notifyFn)
        local hrp0 = hrpOf()
        if not hrp0 then return false end
        local route = Fish.route()
        trace(string.format("go to (%.0f, %.0f, %.0f)", pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z))
        releaseHold()
        if Travel and Travel.rampDrive and #route >= 2 then
            local hereUp, thereUp = isUp(hrp0.Position), isUp(pos)
            if math.abs(route[#route].Y - route[1].Y) >= 6 then
                if thereUp and not hereUp then return goUp(pos, route, keep, notifyFn) end
                if hereUp and not thereUp then return goDown(pos, route, keep, notifyFn) end
            end
        end
        return goToPlain(pos, keep, notifyFn)
    end
    goToPlain = function(pos, keep, notifyFn)
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return false end
        local d = (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude
        local onDock = isUp(hrp.Position) and isUp(pos) and d <= NEAR_DIST
        -- on the dock, far (fishing spot / shop <-> sell): ramp end -> car -> drive -> hop out
        if Travel and onDock and d > DOCK_CAR_DIST and Fish.pastPark and Fish.pastPark(pos) then
            Fish.status = "🚶 Walking (the car stays parked out of the safe zone)…"
            return dockWalk(pos, keep)
        end
        if Travel and onDock and d > DOCK_CAR_DIST then
            local top = rampTop()
            if flatD(hrp.Position, top) > 12 then
                if flatD(hrp.Position, top) <= DOCK_CAR_DIST or flatD(hrp.Position, Fish.spot()) < 45 then
                    carSpot(keep)                                        -- close: run to the ramp end
                else
                    Fish.status = "🚗 Driving to the end of the ramp…"
                    drivePts({ top }, keep, notifyFn, "dock -> ramp end")  -- far (sell pad): drive there
                end
            end
            if not keep() then Travel.hopOut(); return false end
            if flatD(top, pos) > DOCK_CAR_DIST then
                -- stop a few studs before the place, on the line from the ramp end
                local f = Vector3.new(pos.X - top.X, 0, pos.Z - top.Z)
                local stopAt = pos - (f.Magnitude > 1 and f.Unit * 6 or Vector3.zero)
                Fish.status = "🚗 Driving…"
                drivePts({ stopAt }, keep, notifyFn, "ramp end -> place")
            end
            Travel.hopOut()
            if not keep() then return false end
            return Farm.glideTo(pos, keep)
        end
        if onDock then return dockWalk(pos, keep) end
        if Travel and d > NEAR_DIST then
            Fish.status = "🚗 Driving to the fishing dock…"
            for attempt = 1, 3 do
                local fin, okT, msgT = false, false, ""
                Travel.trip(pos, nil, notifyFn, true, function(o, m) okT, msgT, fin = o, m, true end)
                while not fin do
                    if not keep() then Travel.cancel() end
                    task.wait(0.25)
                end
                if string.find(tostring(msgT), "died", 1, true) then return false end
                if okT or not keep() or string.find(tostring(msgT), "Stop", 1, true) then break end
                if attempt == 3 then
                    Fish.status = "⏳ Waiting for your car (standing still)…"
                    local release = Travel.holdStill()
                    local t0 = os.clock()
                    while keep() and os.clock() - t0 < 10 do task.wait(0.25) end
                    release()
                    return false
                end
                task.wait(1.5)
            end
            if not keep() then return false end
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
        return Farm.glideTo(pos, keep)
    end
    -- the right height to stand at over the ground under pos (nil = no ground found)
    local function onTop(pos)
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not (hrp and hum) then return nil end
        local rp = RaycastParams.new()
        rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        rp.IgnoreWater = false
        local ign = {}
        for _, pl in ipairs(P:GetPlayers()) do
            if pl.Character then ign[#ign + 1] = pl.Character end
            local c = workspace:FindFirstChild(pl.Name .. "sCar")
            if c then ign[#ign + 1] = c end
        end
        rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = ign
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(pos + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0), Vector3.new(0, -14, 0), rp)
        if not hit or (hit.Instance == workspace.Terrain and hit.Material == Enum.Material.Water) then return nil end
        local stand = (hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6) and 3 or (hum.HipHeight + hrp.Size.Y / 2)
        return Vector3.new(pos.X, hit.Position.Y + stand + 0.05, pos.Z)
    end
    Fish.onTop = onTop
    -- stand still on pos, facing dir (until the returned fn is called)
    local function pin(pos, dir)
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return function() end end
        local beach = Fish.zoneN() > 1
        if beach then
            Farm.pauseNoclip(true)                              -- real collisions: you stand ON the sand
            task.wait()
            pos = onTop(pos) or pos                             -- beach levels: on top of the sand, never in it
        end
        local cf = dir and CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + dir) or (CFrame.new(pos) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation)
        local hum = L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local oldAR = hum and hum.AutoRotate
        if hum then hum.AutoRotate = false end
        SESSION.tp(hrp, cf, "fishPin")
        local c = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not hrp.Parent then return end
            if (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude > 0.6 or dir then SESSION.tp(hrp, cf, "fishPin") end
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
        return function()
            c:Disconnect()
            if hum and hum.Parent and oldAR ~= nil then hum.AutoRotate = oldAR end
            if beach then Farm.pauseNoclip(false) end
        end
    end
    releaseHold = function() if holdRelease then pcall(holdRelease); holdRelease = nil end end
    holdAt = function(pos, dir) releaseHold(); holdRelease = pin(pos, dir) end
    Fish.releaseHold = releaseHold
    local function waitFor(cond, t, keep)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < t do
            if cond() then return true end
            if keep and not keep() then return false end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        return cond()
    end

    -- ── fishing levels (zones) — from the game's GuideZones message ──
    Fish.ZONES = {
        { n = 1, lvl = 1,  c = Vector3.new(-1459.8, 370.3, 380.8),  s = Vector3.new(535.9, 41.2, 298.2) },
        { n = 2, lvl = 10, c = Vector3.new(-1464.8, 369.8, 684.1),  s = Vector3.new(545.9, 41.3, 308.2) },
        { n = 3, lvl = 20, c = Vector3.new(-1422.0, 368.8, 1040.2), s = Vector3.new(460.9, 43.9, 403.2) },
        { n = 4, lvl = 40, c = Vector3.new(-1428.0, 371.1, 1413.4), s = Vector3.new(474.0, 38.2, 343.2) },
        { n = 5, lvl = 55, c = Vector3.new(-1428.3, 356.4, 1758.5), s = Vector3.new(473.4, 63.2, 353.6) },
    }
    Fish.unlocked = {}
    Fish.level = nil
    Fish.auto = {}                                         -- spots found by "Find Spot" (this session)
    -- level 1: the pier spot you fished from (your cast landed at -1459, 352, 289)
    local PIER1     = Vector3.new(-1453.0, 396.9, 307.5)
    local PIER1_DIR = Vector3.new(-6.3, 0, -18.0).Unit
    local PIER1_POW = 0.03
    function Fish.zoneBox(n)
        local z = Fish.ZONES[n] or Fish.ZONES[1]
        local part = workspace:FindFirstChild("PescaNivel" .. z.n)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return part.Position, part.Size end
        return z.c, z.s
    end
    function Fish.inZone(p, n)
        local c, sz = Fish.zoneBox(n)
        return math.abs(p.X - c.X) <= sz.X / 2 and math.abs(p.Z - c.Z) <= sz.Z / 2
    end
    -- nil = don't know
    -- your REAL level right now (Stats.Level), so levelling up mid-session unlocks the area straight away
    function Fish.myLevel()
        local v
        pcall(function()
            for _, fn in ipairs({ "Stats", "leaderstats" }) do
                local st = L:FindFirstChild(fn)
                local lv = st and st:FindFirstChild("Level")
                if lv and tonumber(lv.Value) then v = tonumber(lv.Value); return end
            end
        end)
        return v or Fish.level
    end
    function Fish.isUnlocked(n)
        if n == 1 then return true end
        local z = Fish.ZONES[n]
        local lv = Fish.myLevel()
        if lv and z and lv >= z.lvl then return true end           -- level high enough = unlocked (old "locked" info ignored)
        if Fish.unlocked[n] ~= nil then return Fish.unlocked[n] == true end
        if lv and z then return false end
        return nil
    end
    -- the level you picked, or the best unlocked one under it
    function Fish.zoneN()
        local n = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(S.FishZone) or 1), 1, #Fish.ZONES)
        while n > 1 and Fish.isUnlocked(n) == false do n = n - 1 end
        return n
    end
    local function parseSpot(str)
        local x, y, z, dx, dz, pw = string.match(tostring(str or ""), "^([%-%d%.]+),([%-%d%.]+),([%-%d%.]+),([%-%d%.]+),([%-%d%.]+),?([%-%d%.]*)$")
        if not x then return nil end
        local dir = Vector3.new(tonumber(dx), 0, tonumber(dz))
        if dir.Magnitude < 0.01 then return nil end
        return Vector3.new(tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z)), dir.Unit, tonumber(pw)
    end
    -- spot for a level: yours (saved) > found > built in (level 1 pier)
    function Fish.zoneSpot(n)
        n = n or Fish.zoneN()
        local p, d, pw = parseSpot(S["FishSpot" .. n])
        if p then return p, d, pw or S.FishPower, "yours" end
        local a = Fish.auto[n]
        if a then return a.p, a.d, a.pw, "found" end
        if n == 1 then return PIER1, PIER1_DIR, PIER1_POW, "pier" end
        return nil
    end
    -- the spot you fish from (current level)
    function Fish.spot()
        local p, d, pw, src = Fish.zoneSpot()
        if p then return p, d, src == "yours", pw end
        return PIER1, PIER1_DIR, false, PIER1_POW
    end
    -- water: first water ahead of p (looking along dir) -> distance, or nil
    local wrp = RaycastParams.new()
    wrp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    wrp.IgnoreWater = false
    local function refreshWrp()
        local ign = {}
        for _, pl in ipairs(P:GetPlayers()) do
            if pl.Character then ign[#ign + 1] = pl.Character end
            local c = workspace:FindFirstChild(pl.Name .. "sCar")
            if c then ign[#ign + 1] = c end
        end
        wrp.FilterDescendantsInstances = ign
    end
    local function probe(x, z, yTop)
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(x, yTop or 470, z), Vector3.new(0, -(yTop or 470) + 300, 0), wrp)
        if not hit then return nil end
        local water = hit.Instance == workspace.Terrain and hit.Material == Enum.Material.Water
        return hit.Position, water
    end
    local function waterAhead(p, dir)
        refreshWrp()
        for d = 3, 150, 2 do
            local q = p + dir * d
            local hp, water = probe(q.X, q.Z, p.Y + 6)
            if hp and water then return d, hp end
        end
        return nil
    end
    -- cast power so the bait lands ~10 studs past the edge of the water (15 + power*135 studs)
    local function powerForDist(d) return math.clamp(((d or 10) + 10 - 15) / 135, 0.01, 1) end
    -- where you stand + the way your camera looks -> spot string
    local function spotHere()
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return nil end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local look = cam and cam.CFrame.LookVector or hrp.CFrame.LookVector
        local dir = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        dir = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        local d = waterAhead(hrp.Position, dir)
        local pw = d and powerForDist(d) or math.clamp(tonumber(S.FishPower) or 0.3, 0.01, 1)
        return hrp.Position, dir, pw, d
    end
    Fish.spotHere = spotHere
    function Fish.setHere()
        local p, dir, pw, d = spotHere()
        if not p then return false end
        local n = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(S.FishZone) or 1), 1, #Fish.ZONES)
        S["FishSpot" .. n] = string.format("%.1f,%.1f,%.1f,%.3f,%.3f,%.3f", p.X, p.Y, p.Z, dir.X, dir.Z, pw)
        return true, n, d
    end
    function Fish.resetSpot()
        local n = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(S.FishZone) or 1), 1, #Fish.ZONES)
        S["FishSpot" .. n] = ""
        Fish.auto[n] = nil
    end
    -- scan a level for a place to stand right at the water (land/pier with water in the zone in front)
    function Fish.findSpot(n, keep)
        keep = keep or function() return ALIVE() end
        refreshWrp()
        local c, sz = Fish.zoneBox(n)
        -- load that area in first (it can be far away)
        for _, off in ipairs({ Vector3.zero, Vector3.new(sz.X / 2, 0, 0), Vector3.new(-sz.X / 2, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, sz.Z / 3), Vector3.new(0, 0, -sz.Z / 3) }) do
            if not keep() then break end
            pcall(function() L:RequestStreamAroundAsync(Vector3.new(c.X + off.X, 360, c.Z + off.Z), 3) end)
        end
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        local me = hrp and hrp.Position or c
        local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
        local x0, x1 = c.X - sz.X / 2, c.X + sz.X / 2
        for zz = c.Z - sz.Z / 2 + 12, c.Z + sz.Z / 2 - 12, 12 do
            if not keep() then break end
            for _, dirX in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
                -- walk across the zone from outside it, looking for land -> water
                local xs, xe = (dirX < 0) and (x1 + 80) or (x0 - 80), (dirX < 0) and x0 or x1
                local lastSolid = nil
                for xx = xs, xe, dirX * 3 do
                    local hp, water = probe(xx, zz)
                    if hp and not water then
                        lastSolid = (hp.Y >= 351 and hp.Y < 425) and hp or nil
                    elseif hp and water then
                        if lastSolid and Fish.inZone(hp, n) then
                            local dir = Vector3.new(dirX, 0, 0)
                            local stand = lastSolid - dir * 2 + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                            local dW = math.abs(hp.X - stand.X)
                            local land = stand + dir * (dW + 10)
                            local lp, lw = probe(land.X, land.Z)
                            if lp and lw and Fish.inZone(land, n) then
                                -- levels 2-5 are down at the BEACH: low ground right at the water wins
                                local dd = (stand - me).Magnitude * 0.25
                                if n > 1 and stand.Y > 381 then dd = dd + 600 end
                                if dd < bestD then best, bestD = { p = stand, d = dir, pw = powerForDist(dW) }, dd end
                            end
                        end
                        lastSolid = nil
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait()
        end
        if best then Fish.auto[n] = best end
        return best
    end

    -- ── shop (rod + bait) ────────────────────────────────────────
    local busy = false
    -- walk (real steps) onto a pad so the game sees you standing on it
    local function walkOnto(pad, keep)
        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not (hum and hrp) then return end
        for _ = 1, 2 do
            hum:MoveTo(Vector3.new(pad.X, hrp.Position.Y, pad.Z))
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while keep() and os.clock() - t0 < 3 and (Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X - pad.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z - pad.Z)).Magnitude > 1.5 do task.wait(0.1) end
            if (Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X - pad.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z - pad.Z)).Magnitude <= 1.5 then return end
        end
    end
    Fish.lastShop = ""
    local function atShop(keep, notifyFn)
        local pad = SHOP_POS
        local since = os.clock()
        goTo(pad + Vector3.new(0, 0, -6), keep, notifyFn)    -- stop next to the pad
        if not keep() then return false, function() end end
        walkOnto(pad, keep)                                  -- then step onto it
        local ok = (shopOpen and shopAt > since) or waitFor(function() return shopOpen and shopAt > since end, 2, keep)
        if not ok then
            -- step off and back on
            local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then hum:MoveTo(pad + Vector3.new(0, 0, -7)); task.wait(1.2) end
            walkOnto(pad, keep)
            ok = waitFor(function() return shopOpen and shopAt > since end, 4, keep)
        end
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        Fish.lastShop = "shop open: " .. tostring(ok) .. "  dist to pad: " .. (hrp and string.format("%.1f", (hrp.Position - pad).Magnitude) or "?")
        trace(Fish.lastShop)
        return ok, pin(hrp and hrp.Position or pad)
    end
    local function closeGui(gn, fn)
        pcall(function()
            local g = pg:FindFirstChild(gn)
            local f = g and g:FindFirstChild(fn)
            if f then f.Visible = false end
        end)
    end
    local function buyOne(item)
        local before = fbAt
        rBuy:FireServer(item)
        waitFor(function() return fbAt > before end, 3)
        return fbAt > before and fbOk, fbMsg
    end
    -- returns true when you have a rod
    function Fish.shop(keep, notifyFn, wantRod, wantBait)
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        if not rBuy then notifyFn("Fishing", "BuyRod remote not found", "x"); return false end
        Fish.status = "🎣 Going to the fishing shop…"
        local ok, unpin = atShop(keep, notifyFn)
        if not keep() then unpin(); return Fish.rodTool() ~= nil end
        if not ok then notifyFn("Fishing", "Shop window didn't show — trying to buy anyway", "shopping-cart") end
        if wantRod and not Fish.rodTool() then
            Fish.status = "Buying the rod…"
            local good, msg = buyOne(S.FishRod)
            if not good then
                -- can't afford the one you picked -> try the cheaper rods
                for i = #Fish.RODS, 1, -1 do
                    local r = Fish.RODS[i].name
                    if r ~= S.FishRod and not Fish.rodTool() then
                        local g2 = buyOne(r)
                        if g2 then good = true; break end
                    end
                end
                if not good then
                    Fish.lastShop = Fish.lastShop .. "  rod buy: " .. tostring(msg)
                    notifyFn("Fishing", "Couldn't buy a rod: " .. (msg ~= "" and tostring(msg) or (ok and "no answer from the shop" or "not on the shop pad")), "x")
                end
            end
            waitFor(function() return Fish.rodTool() ~= nil end, 2)
        end
        if wantBait then
            Fish.status = "Buying bait…"
            local want = math.clamp(tonumber(S.FishBaitAmount) or 5, 1, 20)
            local bought, misses, silent = 0, 0, 0
            -- FAST + never wasted: the next buy goes out the instant the shop answers the last one.
            -- (Spamming all at once made the server ignore most of them -> it ended up ~1 per second.)
            -- If the shop stops answering, it learns the shortest gap that works and remembers it.
            local gap = Fish.baitGap or 0
            local function lowerMsg() return string.lower(fbMsg or "") end
            while bought < want and keep() and misses < 3 do
                local before = fbAt
                pcall(function() rBuy:FireServer(S.FishBait) end)
                waitFor(function() return fbAt > before end, 0.8)
                if fbAt > before then
                    silent = 0
                    if fbOk then
                        bought = bought + 1
                        Fish.status = "Buying bait… " .. bought .. "/" .. want
                        if gap > 0 then task.wait(gap) end
                    else
                        local m = lowerMsg()
                        if string.find(m, "cash", 1, true) or string.find(m, "money", 1, true)
                            or string.find(m, "afford", 1, true) or string.find(m, "enough", 1, true) then
                            notifyFn("Fishing", "Bait: " .. tostring(fbMsg), "x")    -- not enough cash
                            break
                        elseif string.find(m, "wait", 1, true) or string.find(m, "fast", 1, true)
                            or string.find(m, "cooldown", 1, true) or string.find(m, "slow", 1, true) then
                            gap = math.min(gap + 0.1, 1); Fish.baitGap = gap            -- the shop wants a short pause
                            task.wait(gap)
                        else
                            misses = misses + 1                                         -- "not at the shop" etc.
                            trace("bait: " .. fbMsg .. " -> re-stepping on the pad")
                            local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            unpin()
                            if hum then hum:MoveTo(SHOP_POS + Vector3.new(0, 0, -7)); task.wait(1) end
                            walkOnto(SHOP_POS, keep)
                            task.wait(0.4)
                            local hrpB = hrpOf()
                            unpin = pin(hrpB and hrpB.Position or SHOP_POS)
                        end
                    end
                else
                    -- no answer at all = the shop ignored it (too fast) -> slightly longer gap next time
                    silent = silent + 1
                    gap = math.min(gap + 0.1, 1); Fish.baitGap = gap
                    if silent >= 4 then
                        silent = 0
                        misses = misses + 1
                        trace("bait: no answer -> re-stepping on the pad")
                        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        unpin()
                        if hum then hum:MoveTo(SHOP_POS + Vector3.new(0, 0, -7)); task.wait(1) end
                        walkOnto(SHOP_POS, keep)
                        task.wait(0.4)
                        local hrpB = hrpOf()
                        unpin = pin(hrpB and hrpB.Position or SHOP_POS)
                    end
                end
            end
            trace("bait bought: " .. bought .. "/" .. want .. "  now have " .. Fish.baitTotal())
        end
        closeGui("FishingShopGui", "ClassicFishingShop")
        unpin()
        return Fish.rodTool() ~= nil
    end

    -- ── sell ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local function fireButton(btn)
        if not btn then return false end
        local fired = false
        for _, sig in ipairs({ "Activated", "MouseButton1Click", "MouseButton1Down", "MouseButton1Up" }) do
            pcall(function()
                local s = btn[sig]
                if getconnections then
                    for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(s)) do
                        if c.Function or c.Fire then pcall(function() c:Fire() end); fired = true end
                    end
                elseif firesignal then
                    firesignal(s); fired = true
                end
            end)
            if fired then break end
        end
        return fired
    end
    local function sold() return Fish.bagCount() == 0 end
    local function fireSell(a)
        mine = true
        pcall(function() rSell:FireServer(table.unpack(a)) end)
        mine = false
    end
    function Fish.sell(keep, notifyFn)
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        local n0 = Fish.bagCount()
        if n0 <= 0 then return true end
        Fish.status = "💰 Going to sell " .. n0 .. " fish…"
        local since = os.clock()
        goTo(SELL_POS + Vector3.new(6, 0, 0), keep, notifyFn)   -- stop next to the pad
        if not keep() then return false end
        walkOnto(SELL_POS, keep)                                  -- then step onto it
        local opened = (sellOpen and sellAt > since) or waitFor(function() return sellOpen and sellAt > since end, 4, keep)
        if not opened then
            local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then hum:MoveTo(SELL_POS + Vector3.new(7, 0, 0)); task.wait(1.2) end
            walkOnto(SELL_POS, keep)
            opened = waitFor(function() return sellOpen and sellAt > since end, 4, keep)
        end
        local hrpS = hrpOf()
        local unpin = pin(hrpS and hrpS.Position or SELL_POS)
        local c0 = cash()
        Fish.status = "💰 Selling…"
        -- 0) the game's real "Sell all" (from your sell dump): Sell:FireServer("ALL", 0)
        if not sold() and rSell then
            fireSell({ "ALL", 0 })
            waitFor(sold, 3, keep)
            if not sold() and keep() then fireSell({ "ALL", 0 }); waitFor(sold, 3, keep) end
        end
        -- 1) the exact args the game used before (learned)
        if not sold() and S.FishSellArgs ~= "" and rSell then
            local okA, a = pcall(function() return Http:JSONDecode(S.FishSellArgs) end)
            if okA and type(a) == "table" then
                fireSell(a)
                waitFor(sold, 2.5, keep)
            end
        end
        -- 2) press the game's own "Sell all" button (the hook learns the args from it)
        if not sold() then
            local g = pg:FindFirstChild("FishingSellGui")
            local sc = g and g:FindFirstChild("SaleControls", true)
            if sc and fireButton(sc:FindFirstChild("SellAll")) then waitFor(sold, 2.5, keep) end
        end
        -- 3) the likely formats
        if not sold() and rSell then
            local tries = { { "All" }, { "all" }, { "SellAll" }, {} }
            for name, cnt in pairs(Fish.bag) do
                if (tonumber(cnt) or 0) > 0 then tries[#tries + 1] = { name, cnt } end
            end
            for _, a in ipairs(tries) do
                if sold() or not keep() then break end
                local nb = Fish.bagCount()
                fireSell(a)
                waitFor(function() return Fish.bagCount() < nb end, 1.5, keep)
                if sold() and #a == 1 then pcall(function() S.FishSellArgs = Http:JSONEncode(a) end) end
            end
        end
        closeGui("FishingSellGui", "ClassicFishingSell")
        unpin()
        local got = cash() - c0
        trace("sell: window " .. tostring(opened) .. "  fish " .. n0 .. " -> " .. Fish.bagCount() .. "  cash +" .. got)
        if Fish.bagCount() < n0 then
            Fish.sold = Fish.sold + math.max(got, 0)
            notifyFn("Fishing", "Sold " .. (n0 - Fish.bagCount()) .. " fish" .. (got > 0 and (" for $" .. got) or ""), "check")
            return true
        end
        notifyFn("Fishing", opened and "Couldn't sell — sell once by hand so I can learn it" or "The sell window didn't open", "x")
        return false
    end

    -- ── one catch ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local function tri(x)
        x = math.abs(x) % 2
        return x <= 1 and x or 2 - x
    end
    local function markerAt(t) return tri((t - st.startedAt) * st.speed) end
    local function serverNow()
        local ok, t = pcall(function() return workspace:GetServerTimeNow() end)
        return ok and t or os.time()
    end
    -- reel until Caught / lost.  returns "caught" | "lost"
    local function reelLoop(keep)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        local lastSent = 0
        while keep() and os.clock() - t0 < 40 do
            local k = st.name
            if k == "Caught" then return "caught" end
            if k ~= "Bite" and k ~= "Miss" and k ~= "Hit" then
                if os.clock() - st.at > 1.5 then return "lost" end
            else
                local pending = os.clock() - lastSent < 0.9 and st.at < lastSent
                if not pending then
                    local T = serverNow()
                    local a, w = st.start, st.width
                    local lo, hi = a + w * 0.15, a + w * 0.85
                    local p1, p2 = markerAt(T), markerAt(T + 0.12)
                    if p1 >= lo and p1 <= hi and p2 >= lo and p2 <= hi and st.token then
                        lastSent = os.clock()
                        pcall(function() rReel:FireServer(st.token, T, (st.hits or 0) + 1) end)
                    end
                end
            end
            R.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
        return "lost"
    end
    local castFails = 0
    local underBlocked = false
    function Fish.resetUnder() underBlocked = false end
    local function standPos()
        local spot, dir = Fish.spot()
        local hrpS = hrpOf()
        if hrpS and Vector3.new(hrpS.Position.X - spot.X, 0, hrpS.Position.Z - spot.Z).Magnitude <= 6 then spot = Vector3.new(spot.X, spot.Y, spot.Z) end
        if false then
            return spot - Vector3.new(0, math.clamp(tonumber(S.FishUnderDepth) or 10, 3, 40), 0), dir, spot
        end
        return spot, dir, spot
    end
    -- underground at the spot -> glide back up first (before driving / walking anywhere)
    function Fish.surface() end   -- underground is gone: never moves you up/down
    -- one test cast from pos facing dir: true if the game accepts it
    function Fish.testCast(pos, dir, power, keep)
        keep = keep or function() return ALIVE() end
        if not Fish.rodTool() then return false, "no rod (buy one first)" end
        local unpin = pin(pos, dir)
        local res, msg = false, "no answer"
        pcall(function()
            if not equipRod() then msg = "couldn't equip the rod"; return end
            task.wait(0.3)
            local nAt, sAt = notifyAt, st.at
            rCast:FireServer(math.clamp(power or 0.6, 0.05, 1))
            waitFor(function() return st.at > sAt or notifyAt > nAt end, 4, keep)
            if st.at > sAt and st.name == "Waiting" then res, msg = true, "accepted"
            elseif notifyAt > nAt then msg = Fish.lastNotify end
        end)
        unpin()
        trace("test cast: " .. msg)
        return res, msg
    end
    local WATER_PT = Vector3.new(-1520, 352, 270)   -- open water in zone 1 (your casts landed around here)
    local function powerFor(p) return math.clamp((Vector3.new(WATER_PT.X - p.X, 0, WATER_PT.Z - p.Z).Magnitude - 15) / 135, 0.05, 1) end
    local function dirFor(p) local f = Vector3.new(WATER_PT.X - p.X, 0, WATER_PT.Z - p.Z); return f.Magnitude > 0.1 and f.Unit or SPOT_DIR end
    -- "Test This Spot": cast once from where you stand, aimed at the open water
    function Fish.testHere(notifyFn)
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return end
        local p = hrp.Position
        local ok, msg = Fish.testCast(p, dirFor(p), powerFor(p))
        local safe = Travel and Travel.inSafeZone and Travel.inSafeZone()
        if ok then
            S.FishSpot = string.format("%.1f,%.1f,%.1f,%.3f,%.3f", p.X, p.Y, p.Z, dirFor(p).X, dirFor(p).Z)
            S.FishPower = powerFor(p)
            notifyFn("Fishing", "✅ This spot works" .. (safe and " (in the SAFE ZONE)" or "") .. " — saved as your spot (Save Config to keep it)", "check")
        else
            notifyFn("Fishing", "❌ Can't fish here: " .. tostring(msg), "x")
        end
    end
    -- walk from the fishing shop toward the water and test spots that are INSIDE the safe zone
    local finding = false
    function Fish.isFinding() return finding end
    function Fish.stopFind() finding = false end
    function Fish.findSafeSpot(notifyFn)
        if finding then return end
        if not Fish.rodTool() then return notifyFn("Fishing", "Get a rod first (Go To Fishing Shop)", "x") end
        finding = true
        local keep = function() return finding and ALIVE() end
        local from, to = SHOP_POS, SPOT
        local line = Vector3.new(to.X - from.X, 0, to.Z - from.Z)
        local right = Vector3.new(-line.Unit.Z, 0, line.Unit.X)
        local steps = math.floor(line.Magnitude / 4)
        local found, tested, lastMsg = false, 0, ""
        Fish.status = "🔎 Looking for a safe-zone spot…"
        for i = 0, steps do
            if not keep() then break end
            local base = from:Lerp(to, i / math.max(steps, 1))
            for _, off in ipairs({ 0, 10, -10 }) do
                if not keep() then break end
                local p = base + right * off
                Farm.glideTo(Vector3.new(p.X, from.Y, p.Z), keep)
                task.wait(0.35)
                local hrp = hrpOf()
                if hrp and Travel.inSafeZone() then
                    tested = tested + 1
                    local hp = hrp.Position
                    Fish.status = "🔎 Testing safe-zone spot #" .. tested .. "…"
                    local ok, msg = Fish.testCast(hp, dirFor(hp), powerFor(hp), keep)
                    lastMsg = msg
                    if ok then
                        S.FishSpot = string.format("%.1f,%.1f,%.1f,%.3f,%.3f", hp.X, hp.Y, hp.Z, dirFor(hp).X, dirFor(hp).Z)
                        S.FishPower = powerFor(hp)
                        found = true
                        break
                    end
                    task.wait(0.8)
                end
            end
            if found then break end
        end
        finding = false
        Fish.status = "Off"
        if found then
            notifyFn("Fishing", "✅ Found a spot INSIDE the safe zone — saved (Save Config to keep it)", "check")
        elseif tested == 0 then
            notifyFn("Fishing", "The safe zone doesn't reach toward the water here", "x")
        else
            notifyFn("Fishing", "❌ Tested " .. tested .. " safe-zone spots, the game said: " .. tostring(lastMsg), "x")
        end
        return found
    end
    local function castOnce(keep, notifyFn)
        local stand, dir, spot = standPos()
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return false end
        if Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X - spot.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z - spot.Z).Magnitude > 4 or math.abs(hrp.Position.Y - spot.Y) > 8 then
            Fish.status = "🎣 Going to the water…"
            releaseHold()
            if not Fish.goFish(keep, notifyFn) then return false end
            stand, dir, spot = standPos()
        end
        local _, _, _, spotPow = Fish.spot()
        -- the game sometimes refuses a cast ("more open spot"…) -> try a bit further / a bit to the side
        local VARS = { { 0, 0 }, { 0.06, 0 }, { 0, -25 }, { 0, 25 }, { 0.15, 0 }, { 0.3, 0 }, { 0.15, -45 }, { 0.15, 45 } }
        local key = string.format("%d|%.0f,%.0f", Fish.zoneN(), spot.X, spot.Z)
        if Fish._varKey ~= key then Fish._varKey, Fish.castVar = key, 1 end
        local v = VARS[Fish.castVar] or VARS[1]
        local a = math.rad(v[2])
        local cdir = Vector3.new(dir.X * math.cos(a) - dir.Z * math.sin(a), 0, dir.X * math.sin(a) + dir.Z * math.cos(a))
        -- your Cast Power slider (10 = red = farthest); only if the game refuses that cast
        -- it falls back to the spot's own power + the small tweaks
        if (Fish.castVar or 1) <= 1 then
            spotPow = math.clamp((tonumber(S.FishCastPower) or 10) / 10, 0.1, 1)
        else
            spotPow = math.clamp((tonumber(spotPow) or tonumber(S.FishPower) or 0.3) + v[1], 0.01, 1)
        end
        if Fish._holdDir ~= cdir then releaseHold(); Fish._holdDir = cdir end
        dir = cdir
        -- stay right at your spot the whole time you fish — no dropping between casts
        hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return false end
        local here = hrp.Position
        if Vector3.new(here.X - spot.X, 0, here.Z - spot.Z).Magnitude <= 6 then here = spot end
        if not holdRelease then holdAt(here, dir) end
        -- keep the bait inside your level's fishing area (a cast that lands outside it never bites)
        do
            local c, sz = Fish.zoneBox(Fish.zoneN())
            local function inside(pw)
                local q = here + dir * (15 + pw * 135)
                return math.abs(q.X - c.X) <= sz.X / 2 - 6 and math.abs(q.Z - c.Z) <= sz.Z / 2 - 6
            end
            while spotPow > 0.1 and not inside(spotPow) do spotPow = spotPow - 0.05 end
        end
        local unpin = function() end
        local res = false
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            if not equipRod() then Fish.status = "No rod"; return end
            task.wait(0.3)
            Fish.status = "🎣 Casting…"
            local nAt, sAt = notifyAt, st.at
            st.name = ""
            rCast:FireServer(math.clamp(tonumber(spotPow) or tonumber(S.FishPower) or 0.3, 0.01, 1))
            waitFor(function() return st.at > sAt or notifyAt > nAt end, 4, keep)
            if st.name ~= "Waiting" then
                castFails = castFails + 1
                if S.FishUnder and not underBlocked and castFails >= 2 then
                    underBlocked = true
                    castFails = 0
                    notifyFn("Fishing", "The game blocks casting from under the floor here (" .. (Fish.lastNotify ~= "" and Fish.lastNotify or "no answer") .. ") — fishing normally", "x")
                end
                if notifyAt > nAt and string.find(string.lower(Fish.lastNotify), "bait", 1, true) then
                    Fish.wantBait = true                         -- the game says: no bait -> go buy some
                elseif not string.find(string.lower(Fish.lastNotify), "full", 1, true) then
                    Fish.castVar = (Fish.castVar or 1) % #VARS + 1   -- next try: a bit further / to the side
                    trace("cast refused -> trying variant " .. Fish.castVar)
                end
                Fish.status = "Cast failed: " .. (notifyAt > nAt and Fish.lastNotify or "no answer")
                trace(Fish.status)
                task.wait(1.5)
                return
            end
            castFails = 0
            Fish.status = "🎣 Waiting for a bite…"
            waitFor(function() return st.name ~= "Waiting" end, 16, keep)
            if st.name == "Bite" then
                Fish.status = "🐟 Bite! Reeling…"
                local r = reelLoop(keep)
                if r == "caught" then
                    Fish.status = "✅ Caught " .. Fish.lastFish
                    res = true
                    task.wait(1.6)   -- catch animation
                else
                    Fish.status = "The fish got away"
                    task.wait(1)
                end
            else
                Fish.status = "No bite — casting again"
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)
        unpin()
        if not ok then Fish.status = "Error: " .. tostring(err); task.wait(1) end
        return res
    end

    -- ── TEST: "Fish" button — cast from right where you stand, the way your
    --    camera faces, at your Cast Power, and auto-reel it. Shows what the game says.
    local testing = false
    function Fish.isTesting() return testing end
    function Fish.testFish(notifyFn)
        if testing then return end
        if not Fish.rodTool() then return notifyFn("Fishing", "No rod — buy one first", "x") end
        if Fish.baitTotal() <= 0 then return notifyFn("Fishing", "No bait — buy some first", "x") end
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return end
        testing = true
        local keep = function() return testing and ALIVE() end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local look = cam and cam.CFrame.LookVector or hrp.CFrame.LookVector
        local dir = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        dir = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        local power = math.clamp((tonumber(S.FishCastPower) or 10) / 10, 0.1, 1)
        local unpin = pin(hrp.Position, dir)
        local result
        pcall(function()
            if not equipRod() then result = "couldn't equip the rod"; return end
            task.wait(0.3)
            local nAt, sAt = notifyAt, st.at
            st.name = ""
            notifyFn("Fishing", "🎣 Test cast (" .. math.floor(15 + power * 135) .. " studs)…", "fish")
            rCast:FireServer(power)
            waitFor(function() return st.at > sAt or notifyAt > nAt end, 4, keep)
            if st.name ~= "Waiting" then
                result = "❌ Cast refused: " .. (notifyAt > nAt and Fish.lastNotify or "no answer")
                return
            end
            notifyFn("Fishing", "✅ Cast accepted — waiting for a bite…", "fish")
            waitFor(function() return st.name ~= "Waiting" end, 20, keep)
            if st.name == "Bite" then
                local r = reelLoop(keep)
                result = (r == "caught") and ("✅ It works from here! Caught " .. Fish.lastFish) or "Bite, but the fish got away (the cast itself works here)"
            else
                result = "✅ Cast works here, but no bite in 20s"
            end
        end)
        unpin()
        testing = false
        trace("test fish: " .. tostring(result))
        notifyFn("Fishing", tostring(result or "stopped"), "fish")
    end
    function Fish.stopTest() testing = false end

    -- ════════ PIER CYCLE ═════════════════════════════════════════
    --   car waits just before the end of the pier (outside the shop's safe zone)
    --   shop + fishing on foot · sell: car -> seller -> sell from the car fast (before the
    --   safe-zone car timer) -> straight back to the park spot
    local function seatedNow()
        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        return hum and hum.SeatPart ~= nil
    end
    -- 🔒 the car ALWAYS waits here: on the ramp at YOUR spot (-1376.82, 389.21, 324.83)
    --    (outside the pier's safe zone -> never despawns).  Old saved spots are ignored.
    local PARK_SPOT = Vector3.new(-1376.82, 389.21, 324.83)   -- 🔒 YOUR spot (out of the safe zone) — always park here
    local function parkPos() return PARK_SPOT end
    Fish.parkPos = parkPos
    -- 🔒 the car NEVER goes past the park spot (end of the ramp / dock = safe zone -> car deleted).
    --    any drive point up on the pier past it becomes the park spot; you walk the rest.
    do
        local dir = Vector3.new(RAMP_END.X - RAMP_START.X, 0, RAMP_END.Z - RAMP_START.Z).Unit
        local parkS = dir:Dot(Vector3.new(PARK_SPOT.X - RAMP_START.X, 0, PARK_SPOT.Z - RAMP_START.Z))
        function Travel.pierClamp(p)
            if typeof(p) ~= "Vector3" then return p, false end
            local sAlong = dir:Dot(Vector3.new(p.X - RAMP_START.X, 0, p.Z - RAMP_START.Z))
            if sAlong > parkS + 1 and p.Y > 375 then return PARK_SPOT, true end
            return p, false
        end
        Fish.pastPark = function(p) local _, c = Travel.pierClamp(p); return c end
    end
    -- ramp route from the bottom, ending at the park spot (not inside the safe zone)
    local function routeToPark()
        local r = Fish.route()
        local park, top = parkPos(), r[#r]
        local dPark = flatD(park, top)
        local out = {}
        for k = 1, #r - 1 do
            if flatD(r[k], top) <= dPark + 2 then break end
            out[#out + 1] = r[k]
        end
        out[#out + 1] = park
        return out
    end
    Fish.routeToPark = routeToPark
    local function sellRoute()
        local pts = {}
        for x, y, z in string.gmatch(tostring(S.FishSellRoute or ""), "([%-%d%.]+),([%-%d%.]+),([%-%d%.]+)") do
            pts[#pts + 1] = Vector3.new(tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z))
        end
        if #pts >= 2 then
            if (pts[1] - parkPos()).Magnitude > 5 then table.insert(pts, 1, parkPos()) end
            return pts, true
        end
        return { parkPos(), RAMP_END + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), SELL_POS }, false   -- park -> top of the ramp -> along the dock
    end
    Fish.sellRoute = sellRoute
    local function rev(t) local o = {} for k = #t, 1, -1 do o[#o + 1] = t[k] end return o end
    -- level 1 spot is on the pier; others are far -> car
    local function pierSpot(p) return Fish.zoneN() == 1 and flatD(p, rampTop()) < 250 and isUp(p) end
    local function onMainDock(p) return isUp(p) and flatD(p, rampTop()) < 400 end
    -- anywhere -> in the car, parked at the park spot
    local function toPark(keep, notifyFn)
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return false end
        local park = parkPos()
        if onMainDock(hrp.Position) then
            if not boardHere(keep, notifyFn) then return false end
            local car = Travel.myCar()
            local okC, cp = pcall(function() return car:GetPivot().Position end)
            if okC and cp and flatD(cp, park) > 6 then
                Travel.rampDrive({ park }, notifyFn)
            end
            return seatedNow()
        end
        Fish.status = "🚗 Driving to the pier…"
        if not carTrip(Fish.route()[1], keep, notifyFn, false) then return false end
        if not keep() then return false end
        Fish.status = "🚗 Driving up the pier…"
        local okP = drivePts(routeToPark(), keep, notifyFn, "pier up -> park")
        return okP
    end
    Fish.toPark = toPark
    -- up on the pier -> down to the bottom of the ramp (drive it; if that fails, walk it)
    local function downRamp(keep, notifyFn)
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp or not onMainDock(hrp.Position) then return true end
        local okD = false
        local car = Travel.myCar and Travel.myCar()
        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if car and car.Parent and not (hum and hum.SeatPart) then
            Fish.status = "🚶 Walking to your car…"
            Travel.runToCar(car)                               -- straight to the car's door (on the ramp)
            if not keep() then return false end
        end
        if boardHere(keep, notifyFn) then
            Fish.status = "🚗 Driving down the ramp…"
            okD = drivePts({ RAMP_START }, keep, notifyFn, "straight down the ramp")
        end
        if not keep() then return false end
        if not okD then
            trace("drive down the ramp failed -> walking down it")
            Travel.hopOut()
            walkRamp(Fish.route(), false, keep)
        end
        local h2 = hrpOf()
        return h2 ~= nil and not onMainDock(h2.Position)
    end
    -- go to your fishing spot for the level you picked
    function Fish.goFish(keep, notifyFn)
        local n = Fish.zoneN()
        local spot, dir = Fish.zoneSpot(n)
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not hrp then return false end
        if not spot then
            -- no spot for this level yet -> look for one
            Fish.status = "🔎 Looking for a spot at level " .. Fish.ZONES[n].lvl .. "…"
            local found = Fish.findSpot(n, keep)      -- loads that area in first, then looks
            if not found then
                local c, sz = Fish.zoneBox(n)
                local guess = Vector3.new(c.X + sz.X / 2 + 20, 366, c.Z)
                if not downRamp(keep, notifyFn) then return false end
                if keep() then carTrip(guess, keep, notifyFn, false) end
                Travel.hopOut()
                found = keep() and Fish.findSpot(n, keep)
            end
            if not found then
                notifyFn("Fishing", "Couldn't find a spot for level " .. Fish.ZONES[n].lvl .. " — stand at the water there, face it and press \"Set Spot Here\"", "x")
                S.FishFarm = false
                return false
            end
            notifyFn("Fishing", "Found a spot for level " .. Fish.ZONES[n].lvl .. " — fishing there", "check")
            spot, dir = Fish.zoneSpot(n)
            hrp = hrpOf()
            if not (spot and hrp) then return false end
        end
        if pierSpot(spot) then
            -- level 1: on the pier
            if not onMainDock(hrp.Position) then
                if not toPark(keep, notifyFn) then return false end
                Travel.hopOut()
            elseif seatedNow() then
                Travel.hopOut()
            end
            if not keep() then return false end
            Fish.status = "🚶 Walking to the water…"
            dockWalk(spot, keep)
        else
            -- other levels: down the ramp first (never off the side of it), then drive there
            if onMainDock(hrp.Position) and flatD(hrp.Position, spot) > 60 then
                if not downRamp(keep, notifyFn) then
                    if keep() then notifyFn("Fishing", "Couldn't get down the ramp — trying again", "x") end
                    return false
                end
            end
            if not keep() then return false end
            trace(string.format("to level %d spot (%.0f, %.0f, %.0f)", n, spot.X, spot.Y, spot.Z))
            local h2 = hrpOf()
            if h2 and flatD(h2.Position, spot) > 40 then
                Fish.status = "🚗 Driving to the level " .. Fish.ZONES[n].lvl .. " beach…"
                local back = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
                back = back.Magnitude > 0.1 and back.Unit or Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0)
                local stopAt = spot - back * 10                 -- on the sand, just behind your spot
                carTrip(stopAt, keep, notifyFn, true, { direct = true, noCurb = true, above = true })
            end
            Travel.hopOut()
            if not keep() then return false end
            Fish.status = "🚶 Walking to the water…"
            Farm.glideTo(spot, keep)
        end
        local h3 = hrpOf()
        return h3 ~= nil and flatD(h3.Position, spot) < 8
    end
    -- sell from the car: park -> seller -> sell fast -> straight back to the park spot
    function Fish.sellTrip(keep, notifyFn)
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        local n0 = Fish.bagCount()
        if n0 <= 0 then return true end
        if not Travel then return Fish.sell(keep, notifyFn) end
        releaseHold()
        Fish.status = "🚗 Going to your car to sell " .. n0 .. " fish…"
        if not toPark(keep, notifyFn) then
            notifyFn("Fishing", "Couldn't get to the car — selling on foot", "footprints")
            Travel.hopOut()
            return Fish.sell(keep, notifyFn)
        end
        if not keep() then return false end
        local pts = sellRoute()
        local c0 = cash()
        Fish.selling = true
        Fish.status = "🚗 Driving to the fish seller…"
        local okD, msgD = Travel.rampDrive(pts, notifyFn, { allowZone = true })
        if not okD then
            Fish.selling = false
            Travel.hopOut()
            notifyFn("Fishing", "Sell drive stopped (" .. tostring(msgD) .. ") — selling on foot", "footprints")
            return Fish.sell(keep, notifyFn)
        end
        -- line your character up on the pad
        local car = Travel.myCar()
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if car and hrp then
            local d = Vector3.new(SELL_POS.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, SELL_POS.Z - hrp.Position.Z)
            if d.Magnitude > 1.2 and d.Magnitude < 12 then pcall(function() car:PivotTo(car:GetPivot() + d) end) end
        end
        local tIn = Travel.zoneIn or os.clock()
        Fish.status = "💰 Selling from the car…"
        local learned
        pcall(function() if S.FishSellArgs ~= "" then learned = Http:JSONDecode(S.FishSellArgs) end end)
        local k = 0
        while keep() and not sold() and os.clock() - tIn < 2.1 do
            k = k + 1
            fireSell((k % 2 == 0 and type(learned) == "table") and learned or { "ALL", 0 })
            waitFor(sold, 0.25, keep)
        end
        local okSold = sold()
        closeGui("FishingSellGui", "ClassicFishingSell")
        -- straight back out (before the car timer) and back to the park spot
        Fish.status = "🚗 Driving back to the end of the pier…"
        local back = rev(pts)
        if okSold then
            Travel.rampDrive(back, notifyFn, { allowZone = true })
        else
            -- the game wants you on foot: park just outside the seller's safe zone, walk in, sell, walk back
            trace("car sell didn't go through -> on foot")
            Travel.rampDrive(back, notifyFn, { allowZone = true, stopOut = true })
            Travel.hopOut()
            if keep() then
                Fish.status = "💰 Selling on foot…"
                Farm.glideTo(SELL_POS + Vector3.new(4, 0, 0), keep)
                walkOnto(SELL_POS, keep)
                local t0 = os.clock()
                local unpin = pin((hrpOf() or { Position = SELL_POS }).Position)
                while keep() and not sold() and os.clock() - t0 < 4 do
                    fireSell({ "ALL", 0 })
                    waitFor(sold, 0.4, keep)
                end
                unpin()
                closeGui("FishingSellGui", "ClassicFishingSell")
                okSold = sold()
                if keep() and boardHere(keep, notifyFn) then Travel.rampDrive(back, notifyFn, { allowZone = true }) end
            end
        end
        Travel.hopOut()
        Fish.selling = false
        local got = cash() - c0
        trace("sell trip: fish " .. n0 .. " -> " .. Fish.bagCount() .. "  cash +" .. got)
        if Fish.bagCount() < n0 then
            Fish.sold = Fish.sold + math.max(got, 0)
            notifyFn("Fishing", "Sold " .. (n0 - Fish.bagCount()) .. " fish" .. (got > 0 and (" for $" .. got) or ""), "check")
            return true
        end
        notifyFn("Fishing", "Couldn't sell — sell once by hand so I can learn it", "x")
        return false
    end
    -- shop: park the car first (outside the safe zone), then walk in
    function Fish.shopTrip(keep, notifyFn, wantRod, wantBait)
        releaseHold()
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if hrp and (not onMainDock(hrp.Position) or seatedNow()) and Travel then
            if toPark(keep, notifyFn) then Travel.hopOut() end
        end
        if not keep() then return Fish.rodTool() ~= nil end
        return Fish.shop(keep, notifyFn, wantRod, wantBait)
    end

    -- off the pier in the car (for the box run): car -> down the pier -> still sitting in it
    function Fish.leaveDock(keep, notifyFn)
        releaseHold()
        local hrp = hrpOf()
        if not (hrp and Travel and onMainDock(hrp.Position)) then return true end
        if not boardHere(keep, notifyFn) then return false end
        Fish.status = "🚗 Driving down the pier…"
        local okP = drivePts(rev(routeToPark()), keep, notifyFn, "pier down (boxes)")
        return okP
    end

    -- ── main loop ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local running = false
    local baitFailUntil, sellFailUntil = 0, 0
    Fish.cycles = 0
    function Fish.start(notifyFn)
        if running then return end
        if not Fish.available then
            if notifyFn then notifyFn("Fishing", "Fishing remotes not found in this game", "x") end
            S.FishFarm = false
            return
        end
        running = true
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        pcall(function() local r = rem("RequestSync"); if r then r:FireServer() end end)
        Fish.wantBait = false
        Farm.setNoclip(true)
        do
            local want = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(S.FishZone) or 1), 1, #Fish.ZONES)
            local n = Fish.zoneN()
            if n ~= want then notifyFn("Fishing", "The level " .. Fish.ZONES[want].lvl .. " spot is locked (you're level " .. tostring(Fish.myLevel() or "?") .. ") — fishing at the level " .. Fish.ZONES[n].lvl .. " spot", "lock") end
        end
        if Travel then
            Travel.trace = trace
            Travel.leaveToward = rampTop()                 -- out of the shop's safe zone = back toward the pier end
            Travel.onSzWarn = function(m)
                trace("SafeZoneCarWarning: " .. tostring(m))
                if not Fish.selling then notifyFn("Fishing", "Game warning: car in a safe zone (" .. tostring(m) .. ")", "triangle-alert") end
            end
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            local keep = function() return S.FishFarm and ALIVE() end
            local noRod = 0
            baitFailUntil, sellFailUntil = 0, 0
            while keep() do
                local ok, err = pcall(function()
                    local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then Fish.status = "Waiting to respawn"; task.wait(1); return end
                    if Fish._char ~= L.Character then
                        local first = Fish._char == nil
                        Fish._char = L.Character
                        if not first then
                            -- you died -> leave the spawn safe zone, then the cycle spawns the car and goes again
                            pcall(releaseHold); holdRelease = nil; Fish._holdDir = nil
                            Fish.selling = false
                            Fish.status = "💀 Respawned — getting out of the spawn safe zone…"
                            local t0 = os.clock()
                            while keep() and not hrpOf() and os.clock() - t0 < 10 do task.wait(0.2) end
                            task.wait(1.2)
                            if keep() and Travel and (Travel.inSafeZone() or L:GetAttribute("SpawnProtected") == true) then
                                Travel.leaveToward = rampTop()
                                pcall(Travel.leaveSafeZone, rampTop(), notifyFn)
                            end
                            trace("respawned -> out of the safe zone, back to fishing")
                            return
                        end
                    end
                    -- bag full (or "full" warning) -> sell
                    -- cycle: fish until the bait is out (or the bag is full) -> drive to sell -> drive back -> shop -> fish
                    local bag = Fish.bagCount()
                    local baitOut = Fish.baitTotal() <= 0
                    local full = bag >= CAPACITY   -- 🔒 always sells at a full bag (18)
                        or (baitOut and bag > 0)
                        or (castFails >= 2 and bag > 0 and string.find(string.lower(Fish.lastNotify), "full", 1, true))
                    if full and S.FishAutoSell and os.clock() > sellFailUntil then
                        Fish.surface(keep)
                        if Fish.sellTrip(keep, notifyFn) then
                            Fish.cycles = Fish.cycles + 1
                            Fish.wantBait = true      -- sold -> back at the park spot -> buy bait -> then fish
                        else
                            sellFailUntil = os.clock() + 120   -- don't loop on a broken sell
                        end
                        castFails = 0
                        return
                    end
                    -- no rod (lost on death) or out of bait -> shop
                    local needRod = Fish.rodTool() == nil
                    local needBait = Fish.baitTotal() <= 0 or Fish.wantBait
                    if needRod or needBait then
                        if needRod and not S.FishBuyRod then
                            Fish.status = "No rod — turn on Auto Buy Rod or buy one"
                            task.wait(2)
                            return
                        end
                        if needBait and os.clock() < baitFailUntil then
                            Fish.status = "⏳ No bait — trying the shop again in " .. math.ceil(baitFailUntil - os.clock()) .. "s"
                            task.wait(1)
                            return
                        end
                        Fish.surface(keep)
                        local gotRod = Fish.shopTrip(keep, notifyFn, needRod, needBait)
                        if needBait and Fish.baitTotal() > 0 then Fish.wantBait = false end
                        if needBait and Fish.baitTotal() <= 0 then
                            baitFailUntil = os.clock() + 30      -- bait is required -> wait, then try again
                            notifyFn("Fishing", "Couldn't buy bait (you need bait to fish) — trying again in 30s", "x")
                        end
                        if not gotRod and needRod then
                            noRod = noRod + 1
                            Fish.status = "Couldn't get a rod (" .. noRod .. "/3)"
                            if noRod >= 3 then notifyFn("Fishing", "No rod — stopping (check your cash)", "x"); S.FishFarm = false end
                            task.wait(3)
                        else
                            noRod = 0
                        end
                        return
                    end
                    -- too many failed casts here -> try a bit more power / tell the user
                    if castFails >= 4 then
                        notifyFn("Fishing", "Can't cast here: " .. (Fish.lastNotify ~= "" and Fish.lastNotify or "no answer") .. " — try \"Set Spot Here\" at the water", "x")
                        castFails = 0
                        task.wait(3)
                    end
                    castOnce(keep, notifyFn)
                    task.wait(math.max(tonumber(S.FarmDelay) or 0.5, 0.3))
                end)
                if not ok then Fish.status = "Error: " .. tostring(err); task.wait(1) end
            end
            pcall(function()
                local t, held = Fish.rodTool()
                if t and held then local h = L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if h then h:UnequipTools() end end
            end)
            releaseHold()
            Fish.status = "Off"
            running = false
        end)
    end
    function Fish.isRunning() return running end
    function Fish.stop()
        S.FishFarm = false
    end
    function Fish.isBusy() return busy end

    function Fish.dump()
        local bag = {}
        for k, v in pairs(Fish.bag) do bag[#bag + 1] = k .. "=" .. tostring(v) end
        local bt = {}
        for k, v in pairs(Fish.baits) do bt[#bt + 1] = k .. "=" .. tostring(v) end
        local spot, dir, custom = Fish.spot()
        local lines = {
            "==== FISHING INFO ====",
            "remotes: cast=" .. tostring(rCast ~= nil) .. " reel=" .. tostring(rReel ~= nil) .. " buy=" .. tostring(rBuy ~= nil) .. " sell=" .. tostring(rSell ~= nil),
            "rod: " .. tostring((Fish.rodTool() or {}).Name) .. "   pick: " .. tostring(S.FishRod) .. "   bait: " .. tostring(S.FishBait),
            "bag (" .. Fish.bagCount() .. "): " .. table.concat(bag, ", "),
            "baits: " .. table.concat(bt, ", "),
            "spot: " .. tostring(spot) .. " dir " .. tostring(dir) .. (custom and " (custom)" or " (dock)") .. "  power " .. tostring(S.FishPower),
            "state: " .. st.name .. "  start " .. string.format("%.3f", st.start) .. " width " .. string.format("%.3f", st.width) .. " speed " .. tostring(st.speed) .. " hits " .. tostring(st.hits),
            "shop open: " .. tostring(shopOpen) .. "  sell open: " .. tostring(sellOpen) .. "  last shop msg: " .. fbMsg,
            "sell args: " .. tostring(S.FishSellArgs),
            "last shop: " .. tostring(Fish.lastShop),
            "ramp route: " .. #Fish.route() .. " points" .. (select(2, Fish.route()) and " (recorded)" or " (built-in)") .. "   underground: " .. tostring(S.FishUnder) .. " (" .. tostring(S.FishUnderDepth) .. ")" .. (underBlocked and " BLOCKED" or ""),
            "notify: " .. Fish.lastNotify,
            "caught " .. Fish.caught .. "  misses " .. Fish.misses .. "  fish value $" .. Fish.earned .. "  sold $" .. Fish.sold,
            "status: " .. Fish.status,
            "---- last steps ----",
            table.concat(TRACE, "\n"),
        }
        local txt = table.concat(lines, "\n")
        local cb = setclipboard or toclipboard or set_clipboard
        if cb then pcall(cb, txt) end
        return cb ~= nil
    end

    if S.FishFarm then task.delay(5, function() if S.FishFarm then Fish.start() end end) end
end

-- ================================================================
-- HIDE NAME: your username / display name shows as "......" on YOUR screen only
-- (overhead name, leaderboard, chat, kill feed, game GUIs...). Other players
-- still see your real name - nothing is sent to the server.
-- ================================================================
local HideName = {}
do
    local MASK = "●●●●●●"
    local CG = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local pats = {}
    -- case-insensitive pattern for a name ("Bob_1" -> "[bB][oO][bB]_1")
    local function ciPattern(s)
        return (string.gsub(s, ".", function(c)
            if string.match(c, "%a") then return "[" .. string.lower(c) .. string.upper(c) .. "]" end
            if string.match(c, "[%^%$%(%)%%%.%[%]%*%+%-%?]") then return "%" .. c end
            return c
        end))
    end
    local function buildPats()
        pats = {}
        local list = { L.Name }
        if L.DisplayName and L.DisplayName ~= L.Name and #L.DisplayName >= 3 then list[#list+1] = L.DisplayName end
        table.sort(list, function(a, b) return #a > #b end)
        for _, n in ipairs(list) do pats[#pats+1] = ciPattern(n) end
    end
    buildPats()

    local orig   = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })   -- label -> real text
    local masked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })   -- label -> text we put there
    local function mask(t)
        local out, total = t, 0
        for _, p in ipairs(pats) do
            local n
            out, n = string.gsub(out, p, MASK)
            total = total + n
        end
        return out, total
    end
    local function apply(lbl)
        if not S.HideName then return end
        local t = lbl.Text
        if masked[lbl] == t then return end
        local out, n = mask(t)
        if n > 0 then
            orig[lbl], masked[lbl] = t, out
            lbl.Text = out
        else
            orig[lbl], masked[lbl] = nil, nil
        end
    end
    local all = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })     -- every text object we watch
    local function hook(d)
        if (d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton")) and not all[d] then
            all[d] = true
            pcall(apply, d)
            d:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() pcall(apply, d) end)
        end
    end

    -- overhead name: Humanoid.DisplayName + the game's own name tag
    -- (HumanoidRootPart.CharacterBillboardGui.PlayerName) -> "......" (local only)
