    local realDisplay = {}
    local tagOrig = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local NAME_LABELS = { playername = true, username = true, displayname = true, nametag = true, name = true, nametext = true, playernametext = true }
    -- every BillboardGui / SurfaceGui anywhere (workspace, PlayerGui, CoreGui) - the game
    -- can keep your name tag outside your character and just point it at your head
    local boards = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local function addBoard(d)
        if d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("SurfaceGui") then boards[d] = true end
    end
    local function isMine(g, char)
        if g:IsDescendantOf(char) then return true end
        local a = g.Adornee
        if a and a:IsDescendantOf(char) then return true end
        local par = g.Parent
        if par and par:IsA("BasePart") and par:IsDescendantOf(char) then return true end
        -- tag models named after you (e.g. workspace.NameTags.YourName)
        local n = g.Parent and g.Parent.Name
        if n == L.Name or n == L.Name .. "Tag" or n == L.Name .. "_Tag" then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function maskLabel(lbl, on)
        if lbl.Name == "DrixHide" then return end
        if on then
            local t = lbl.Text
            if t == MASK or t == "" then return end
            local out, hits = mask(t)
            local low = string.lower(t)
            local byName = NAME_LABELS[string.lower(lbl.Name)] ~= nil
            local exact = low == string.lower(L.DisplayName) or low == "@" .. string.lower(L.Name) or low == string.lower(L.Name)
            if hits > 0 or exact or (byName and not string.find(low, "lv", 1, true) and not string.find(low, "level", 1, true)) then
                if tagOrig[lbl] == nil then tagOrig[lbl] = t end
                -- keep the level: "[Lv 12] Name" -> "[Lv 12] ......"
                local put = (hits > 0 and not exact) and out or MASK
                masked[lbl] = put
                lbl.Text = put
            end
        elseif tagOrig[lbl] then
            if lbl.Text == masked[lbl] or lbl.Text == MASK then lbl.Text = tagOrig[lbl] end
            tagOrig[lbl] = nil
            masked[lbl] = nil
        end
    end
    -- this game's tag: HumanoidRootPart.CharacterBillboardGui.PlayerName
    -- The game keeps re-writing that label, so instead of fighting over its text we
    -- make the real text invisible and put our own big dots on top of it (a child
    -- label the game doesn't know about). Checked every frame, so it never comes back.
    -- LevelText / LevelImage / icons are left alone so your level still shows.
    local RunS = game:GetService("RunService")
    local tag = { nextFind = 0 }
    local function findTagLabel(char)
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local cbg = hrp and hrp:FindFirstChild("CharacterBillboardGui")
        if not cbg then
            for _, g in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                local x = g:FindFirstChild("CharacterBillboardGui")
                if x then cbg = x; break end
            end
        end
        local lbl = cbg and cbg:FindFirstChild("PlayerName", true)
        if lbl and (lbl:IsA("TextLabel") or lbl:IsA("TextButton")) then return lbl end
        return nil
    end
    local function unTag()
        local t = tag
        tag = { nextFind = 0 }
        if t.over then pcall(function() t.over:Destroy() end) end
        if t.lbl and t.lbl.Parent then
            pcall(function() t.lbl.TextTransparency = t.tt or 0; t.lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = t.st or 1 end)
        end
    end
    local function makeOver(lbl)
        local o = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        o.Name = "DrixHide"
        o.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        o.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        o.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        o.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1.6)            -- taller box = bigger dots
        o.Text = MASK
        o.TextScaled = true
        o.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        o.TextColor3 = lbl.TextColor3
        o.TextStrokeColor3 = lbl.TextStrokeColor3
        o.TextStrokeTransparency = math.min(tag.st or 1, 0.4)
        o.ZIndex = lbl.ZIndex + 1
        o.Parent = lbl
        return o
    end
    local function tagStep()
        if not S.HideName then
            if tag.lbl or tag.over then unTag() end
            return
        end
        local char = L.Character
        if not char then return end
        local lbl = tag.lbl
        if not (lbl and lbl.Parent and lbl:IsDescendantOf(char)) then
            if os.clock() < tag.nextFind then return end
            tag.nextFind = os.clock() + 0.2
            if tag.over then pcall(function() tag.over:Destroy() end) end
            tag.over = nil
            lbl = findTagLabel(char)
            tag.lbl = lbl
            if not lbl then return end
            tag.tt, tag.st = lbl.TextTransparency, lbl.TextStrokeTransparency
            if tag.tt >= 1 then tag.tt = 0 end
        end
        if lbl.TextTransparency ~= 1 then lbl.TextTransparency = 1 end
        if lbl.TextStrokeTransparency ~= 1 then lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 1 end
        local o = tag.over
        if not (o and o.Parent == lbl) then
            if o then pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
            tag.over = makeOver(lbl)
            o = tag.over
        end
        if o.Text ~= MASK then o.Text = MASK end
        if o.TextColor3 ~= lbl.TextColor3 then o.TextColor3 = lbl.TextColor3 end
    end
    local tagConn = nil
    local function doGameTag(_, on)
        if on then
            if not tagConn then local tn = 0; tagConn = RunS.Heartbeat:Connect(function() if S.PerfMode or S.FPSBoost then tn = tn + 1; if tn % 2 == 1 then return end end pcall(tagStep) end) end
            pcall(tagStep)
        else
            if tagConn then tagConn:Disconnect(); tagConn = nil end
            unTag()
        end
    end
    local function doOverhead(on)
        local char = L.Character
        if not char then return end
        pcall(doGameTag, char, on)
        local seen = {}
        local function doGui(g)
            if seen[g] then return end
            seen[g] = true
            for _, lbl in ipairs(g:GetDescendants()) do
                if lbl:IsA("TextLabel") or lbl:IsA("TextButton") or lbl:IsA("TextBox") then pcall(maskLabel, lbl, on) end
            end
        end
        for _, g in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if g:IsA("BillboardGui") or g:IsA("SurfaceGui") then doGui(g) end
        end
        for g in pairs(boards) do
            if g.Parent and isMine(g, char) then doGui(g) end
        end
        if not on then
            for lbl in pairs(tagOrig) do pcall(maskLabel, lbl, false) end
        end
    end
    local function doHumanoid(on)
        pcall(doOverhead, on)
        local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        if on then
            if hum.DisplayName ~= MASK then
                realDisplay[hum] = hum.DisplayName
                hum.DisplayName = MASK
            end
        elseif realDisplay[hum] then
            hum.DisplayName = realDisplay[hum]
            realDisplay[hum] = nil
        end
    end

    local started = false
    local function start()
        if started then return end
        started = true
        local roots = { L:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") }
        pcall(function() roots[#roots+1] = CG end)
        pcall(function() if gethui then local h = gethui(); if h ~= CG then roots[#roots+1] = h end end end)
        for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
            pcall(function()
                root.DescendantAdded:Connect(hook)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local n = 0
                    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                        n = n + 1
                        if n % 800 == 0 then task.wait() end
                        hook(d)
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end
        -- name tags / billboards in the world (only GUI text, cheap class check)
        workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") then task.defer(hook, d)
            elseif d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("SurfaceGui") then addBoard(d); if S.HideName then task.delay(0.2, function() pcall(doOverhead, true) end) end end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            local n = 0
            for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                n = n + 1
                if n % 1500 == 0 then task.wait() end
                if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") then hook(d) else addBoard(d) end
            end
        end)
        for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
            pcall(function()
                root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                    if d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("SurfaceGui") then addBoard(d); if S.HideName then task.delay(0.2, function() pcall(doOverhead, true) end) end end
                end)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local n = 0
                    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                        n = n + 1
                        if n % 1000 == 0 then task.wait() end
                        addBoard(d)
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end
        L.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(0.5)
            if S.HideName then doHumanoid(true) end
        end)
        L:GetPropertyChangedSignal("DisplayName"):Connect(buildPats)
        -- keep the name above your head hidden (tags get rebuilt on respawn / level up)
        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                task.wait(0.5)
                if S.HideName then pcall(doHumanoid, true) end
            end
        end)
    end

    function HideName.set(on)
        S.HideName = on
        if on then
            start()
            doHumanoid(true)
            -- re-mask everything we already watch
            task.spawn(function()
                local n = 0
                for lbl in pairs(all) do
                    n = n + 1
                    if n % 800 == 0 then task.wait() end
                    pcall(apply, lbl)
                end
            end)
        else
            doHumanoid(false)
            for lbl, real in pairs(orig) do
                pcall(function() if lbl.Text == masked[lbl] then lbl.Text = real end end)
            end
            orig   = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
            masked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        end
    end

    if S.HideName then task.defer(HideName.set, true) end
end

-- ================================================================
-- UI  (WindUI)
-- ================================================================
local VP = (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize) or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
if VP.X < 200 or VP.Y < 200 then VP = Vector2.new(1280, 720) end   -- camera not ready yet
local IS_TABLET = UIS.TouchEnabled and math.min(VP.X, VP.Y) >= 600  -- iPad
local WIN_W = IS_TABLET and math.clamp(VP.X - 160, 580, 760) or (IS_MOBILE and math.clamp(VP.X - 60, 380, 580) or 640)
local WIN_H = IS_TABLET and math.clamp(VP.Y - 160, 360, 500) or (IS_MOBILE and math.clamp(VP.Y - 50, 250, 360) or 440)
local function keyOf(n) local ok, k = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[n] end); return ok and k or nil end
local function menuKeyCode() return keyOf(S.MenuKey) or Enum.KeyCode.RightShift end
local Window = WindUI:CreateWindow({
    Title  = "z8km",
    Author = IS_TABLET and "Farm Hub  •  iPad" or IS_MOBILE and "Farm Hub  •  Mobile" or ("Farm Hub  •  PC  •  " .. S.MenuKey .. " = menu"),
    Icon   = "package",
    Folder = "z8kmFarm",
    Size   = UDim2.fromOffset(WIN_W, WIN_H),
    MinSize = Vector2.new(360, 240),
    MaxSize = Vector2.new(860, 580),
    Transparent  = true,
    Theme        = "Dark",
    Resizable    = true,
    SideBarWidth = IS_TABLET and 200 or (IS_MOBILE and 170 or 200),
    ToggleKey    = menuKeyCode(),
    NewElements  = true,
    HideSearchBar    = false,
    ScrollBarEnabled = false,
    User = { Enabled = false },
    Topbar = { Height = 44, ButtonsType = "Default" },
    OpenButton = {
        Title = "z8km",
        CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0),
        StrokeThickness = 2,
        Enabled = true,
        Draggable = true,
        OnlyMobile = false,
        Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), Color3.fromRGB(170,170,170)),
    },
})
pcall(function() Window:SetToggleKey(menuKeyCode()) end)
pcall(function()
    Window:Tag({ Title = "FARM", Color = Color3.fromHex("#b3141b"), Border = true })
end)

local UI = {}
local NOTIF_ICON_ID = 0
local SG = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local notifIcon = (NOTIF_ICON_ID ~= 0) and ("rbxassetid://" .. NOTIF_ICON_ID)
    or ("rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. L.UserId .. "&w=150&h=150")
-- ── PULL-BACK WATCHER ─────────────────────────────────────────────
-- the server moved you back (rubber-band) while walking -> spam jump for ~1.5s to get out of it.
-- "Pull-back Debug" (Misc) shows a notification each time with what the script did right before.
do
    local lastP, lastT, lastNote = nil, os.clock(), 0
    -- AUTO SPRINT while the script walks you (same as pressing the game's sprint button)
    SESSION.sprintBtn = nil
    local function findSprintBtn()
        local b = SESSION.sprintBtn
        if b and b.Parent and b.Visible then return b end
        SESSION.sprintBtn = nil
        local pg = L:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return nil end
        for _, d in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("GuiButton") and d.Visible then
                local n = string.lower(d.Name .. " " .. (d.Parent and d.Parent.Name or "")
                    .. " " .. ((d:IsA("TextButton") and d.Text) or ""))
                if string.find(n, "sprint") then
                    SESSION.sprintBtn = d
                    return d
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function pressSprint()
        local b = findSprintBtn()
        if b and getconnections then
            for _, sig in ipairs({ "Activated", "MouseButton1Click", "MouseButton1Down" }) do
                local ok, cons = pcall(getconnections, b[sig])
                if ok and cons and #cons > 0 then
                    for _, c in ipairs(cons) do pcall(function() c:Fire() end) end
                    return "button"
                end
            end
        end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, false, game)
        end)
        if ok then SESSION.shiftHeld = true; return "shift" end
        return nil
    end
    local function releaseShift()
        if SESSION.shiftHeld then
            SESSION.shiftHeld = false
            pcall(function() game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, false, game) end)
        end
    end
    local sprT, sprTries, sprAsk = 0, 0, 0
    local function autoSprint(hum, walking)
        local now = os.clock()
        if not walking then
            sprTries = 0
            if SESSION.shiftHeld then releaseShift() end
            return
        end
        if now - sprT < 0.4 then return end
        sprT = now
        if L:GetAttribute("StaminaExhausted") == true then return end
        if hum.WalkSpeed >= 19 then sprAsk = 0; return end                 -- already sprinting
        local tool = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if tool then return end                                           -- holding something (box/mop) -> slower anyway
        pcall(function() L:SetAttribute("SprintRequested", true); L:SetAttribute("MobileSprint", true) end)
        if sprAsk == 0 then sprAsk = now; return end
        if now - sprAsk > 1.2 and sprTries < 2 then                       -- attributes alone didn't do it -> press the button
            sprTries = sprTries + 1
            sprAsk = now
            pressSprint()
        end
    end

    R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local char = L.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local now = os.clock()
        if not (hrp and hum) or hum.SeatPart or hum.Health <= 0 then lastP = nil; autoSprint(nil, false); return end
        autoSprint(hum, (SESSION.autoWalk or 0) > 0)
        local p = hrp.Position
        if lastP then
            local step = Vector3.new(p.X - lastP.X, 0, p.Z - lastP.Z).Magnitude
            if step > 2.5 and step > math.max(hum.WalkSpeed, 16) * (now - lastT) * 2.5 + 1.5
                and now - (SESSION.lastTPt or -100) > 0.3 then
                SESSION.pulls = (SESSION.pulls or 0) + 1
                SESSION.lastPullT = now
                if (SESSION.autoWalk or 0) > 0 then SESSION.jumpUntil = now + 1.5 end   -- 🔒 always on
                if now - lastNote > 1.5 then              -- 🔒 debug always on
                    lastNote = now
                    local why = SESSION.lastTP and now - (SESSION.lastTPt or -100) < 3
                        and (" | script moved you " .. string.format("%.1f", now - SESSION.lastTPt) .. "s ago (" .. tostring(SESSION.lastTP) .. ")") or ""
                    pcall(function()
                        SG:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "Pull-back #" .. SESSION.pulls,
                            Text = string.format("%.1f studs back", step) .. ((SESSION.autoWalk or 0) > 0 and " → jumping out" or "") .. why, Duration = 3 })
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
        if now < (SESSION.jumpUntil or 0) and (SESSION.autoWalk or 0) > 0 and hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
            hum.Jump = true
        end
        lastP, lastT = p, now
    end)
end

local function notify(t, c, img)
    task.spawn(function()
        for _ = 1, 10 do
            local ok = pcall(SG.SetCore, SG, "SendNotification", {
                Title = tostring(t), Text = tostring(c), Icon = notifIcon, Duration = 4,
            })
            if ok then return end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
        pcall(function() WindUI:Notify({ Title = t, Content = c, Duration = 3, Image = img or "info" }) end)
    end)
end

-- ── FARM ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ── FARM CAR (locked) - the same pick in the Farm, Both and Travel tabs ──
local FarmCar = {}
do
    local syncing = false
    local LOADING = "(loading your cars…)"
    -- ONLY the cars this account owns (from your inventory)
    function FarmCar.values()
        local owned = Travel.ownedCars()
        if #owned > 0 then return owned end
        return { LOADING }
    end
    function FarmCar.value()
        local owned = Travel.ownedCars()
        if S.TravelCar ~= "" and table.find(owned, S.TravelCar) then return S.TravelCar end
        return owned[1] or LOADING
    end
    function FarmCar.set(v, from)
        if syncing then return end
        if typeof(v) == "table" then v = v.Title or v[1] end
        v = tostring(v or "")
        if v == "" or v == "nil" or v == LOADING then return end
        local changed = S.TravelCar ~= v
        S.TravelCar = v
        syncing = true
        for _, k in ipairs({ "TravelCar", "FarmCar", "FarmCar2" }) do
            if k ~= from and UI[k] then pcall(function() UI[k]:Select(v) end) end
        end
        syncing = false
        if changed then notify("Car", "Locked to your " .. v .. " — farms only use this car", "car") end
    end
end

-- ================================================================
-- FISH + BOXES  (fish until green / gold boxes are in stock -> car -> boxes -> back -> fish)
-- ================================================================
local FB = { status = "Off", phase = "", cycles = 0 }
do
    local running = false
    local function on() return S.FishBoxFarm and ALIVE() end
    local function waitUntil(cond, maxT)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while on() and not cond() and os.clock() - t0 < (maxT or 20) do task.wait(0.25) end
    end
    local function wantedInStock()
        return ((Farm.delivered.green or 0) < S.BothGreen and Farm.readyKind("green"))
            or ((Farm.delivered.gold or 0) < S.BothGold and Farm.readyKind("gold"))
    end
    local function anyInStock()
        return (S.BothGreen > 0 and Farm.readyKind("green")) or (S.BothGold > 0 and Farm.readyKind("gold"))
    end
    local function nearGas()
        local hrp = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        return hrp ~= nil and (hrp.Position - Farm.gasPoint()).Magnitude < 250
    end
    -- the box stations are far from the water -> ask the game to load that area so we can see their timers
    local lastStream = 0
    local function peekBoxes()
        if os.clock() - lastStream < 15 then return end
        lastStream = os.clock()
        task.spawn(function() pcall(function() L:RequestStreamAroundAsync(Farm.gasPoint(), 3) end) end)
    end

    local function fishPhase(notifyFn)
        FB.phase = "🎣 Fishing"
        S.FishFarm = true
        Fish.start(notifyFn)
        local unknownSince = nil
        while on() do
            peekBoxes()
            if Farm.isCarrying() or anyInStock() then break end
            local c = Farm.counts()
            local seen = (c.green and c.green.t > 0) or (c.gold and c.gold.t > 0)
            if seen then unknownSince = nil else
                unknownSince = unknownSince or os.clock()
                if os.clock() - unknownSince > 480 then break end   -- can't see the boxes from here -> go check every 8 min
            end
            local function line(e) return (not e or e.t == 0) and "?" or e.r > 0 and "✅" or (e.timer or "⏳") end
            FB.status = "🎣 Fishing until boxes are in stock  (🟢 " .. line(c.green) .. "  🟡 " .. line(c.gold) .. ")\n" .. tostring(Fish.status)
            task.wait(1)
        end
        if not on() then return end
        -- let the fish on the line / the sell trip finish first
        FB.status = "🎣 Finishing up before the box run…"
        waitUntil(function()
            local st = tostring(Fish.status)
            return not Fish.selling and not string.find(st, "Reeling", 1, true) and not string.find(st, "Bite", 1, true)
        end, 25)
        S.FishFarm = false
        waitUntil(function() return not Fish.isRunning() end, 15)
        if Fish.releaseHold then pcall(Fish.releaseHold) end
        notifyFn("Fish + Boxes", "Boxes are in stock — driving there", "car")
    end

    local function boxPhase(notifyFn)
        FB.phase = "📦 Boxes"
        -- off the pier in the car first (the car never drives off the edge)
        FB.status = "🚗 Leaving the pier…"
        pcall(Fish.leaveDock, on, notifyFn)
        if not on() then return end
        Farm.delivered = { green = 0, gold = 0, normal = 0 }
        Farm.quota = { green = S.BothGreen, gold = S.BothGold, normal = 0 }
        S.BoxFarm = true
        Farm.start(notifyFn)
        local emptySince
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while on() do
            local carrying = Farm.isCarrying()
            local done = (Farm.delivered.green or 0) >= S.BothGreen and (Farm.delivered.gold or 0) >= S.BothGold
            if done and not carrying then break end
            -- only call it "out of stock" once we're actually there
            if not carrying and nearGas() and not wantedInStock() then
                emptySince = emptySince or os.clock()
                if os.clock() - emptySince > 5 then break end
            else
                emptySince = nil
            end
            if os.clock() - t0 > 600 then break end
            FB.status = string.format("📦 Boxes  🟢 %d/%d  🟡 %d/%d\n%s", Farm.delivered.green, S.BothGreen, Farm.delivered.gold, S.BothGold, Farm.status)
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
        S.BoxFarm = false
        Farm.quota = nil
        waitUntil(function() return not Farm.isRunning() end, 15)
        if on() then notifyFn("Fish + Boxes", string.format("Boxes done (🟢 %d  🟡 %d) — back to fishing", Farm.delivered.green, Farm.delivered.gold), "fish") end
    end

    function FB.start(notifyFn)
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        if running then return end
        running = true
        S.FishBoxFarm = true
        Farm.setNoclip(true)
        task.spawn(function()
            waitUntil(function() return not Farm.isRunning() and not Mop.isRunning() and not Fish.isRunning() end, 15)
            while on() do
                local ok, err = pcall(function()
                    local hum = L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then FB.status = "Waiting to respawn"; task.wait(1); return end
                    peekBoxes()
                    task.wait(0.3)
                    if Farm.isCarrying() or anyInStock() then
                        boxPhase(notifyFn)
                        FB.cycles = FB.cycles + 1
                    end
                    if not on() then return end
                    fishPhase(notifyFn)
                    if not on() then return end
                    boxPhase(notifyFn)
                    FB.cycles = FB.cycles + 1
                end)
                if not ok then FB.status = "Error: " .. tostring(err); task.wait(1) end
            end
            running = false
        end)
    end
    function FB.stop()
        S.FishBoxFarm = false
        Farm.quota = nil
        S.BoxFarm, S.FishFarm = false, false
        Farm.stop(); Fish.stop()
        pcall(function() if Travel.isBusy() then Travel.cancel() end end)
        FB.status, FB.phase = "Off", ""
    end
    function FB.isRunning() return running end
    if S.FishBoxFarm then
        S.FishFarm, S.BoxFarm = false, false
        task.delay(4, function() if S.FishBoxFarm then FB.start(notify) end end)
    end
end

local FarmTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "Farm", Icon = "package" })
local function farmStatus()
    local c = Farm.counts()
    local function line(icon, name, e)
        if not e or e.t == 0 then return icon .. " " .. name .. ": not found (go to the gas station)" end
        if e.r > 0 then return icon .. " " .. name .. ": ✅ ready" end
        return icon .. " " .. name .. ": ⏳ " .. (e.timer or "waiting")
    end
    local s = line("🟢", "Green", c.green) .. "\n" .. line("🟡", "Gold", c.gold) .. "\n" .. line("📦", "Normal", c.normal)
    if Farm.buff ~= "" then s = s .. "\n" .. Farm.buff end
    return s .. "\nFarm: " .. Farm.status
end
local farmPara = FarmTab:Paragraph({ Title = "Delivery Job", Desc = "Scanning…" })
FarmTab:Space()
UI.BoxFarm = FarmTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Farm Boxes", Desc = "Picks up boxes, stands on the blue circle, repeats (noclip is always on)",
    Type = "Checkbox", Value = S.BoxFarm, Default = S.BoxFarm,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v and S.FishFarm then Fish.stop(); pcall(function() UI.FishFarm:Set(false) end) end
        if v and S.BothFarm then Both.stop(); pcall(function() UI.BothFarm:Set(false) end) end
        if v and S.FishBoxFarm then FB.stop(); pcall(function() UI.FishBoxFarm:Set(false) end) end
        S.BoxFarm = v
        if v and S.MopFarm then Mop.stop(); pcall(function() UI.MopFarm:Set(false) end) end
        if v then Farm.start(notify) else Farm.stop(); pcall(function() if Travel.isBusy() then Travel.cancel() end end) end
    end,
})
FarmTab:Space()
FarmTab:Section({ Title = "Which Boxes", TextSize = 14 })
UI.FarmGreen = FarmTab:Toggle({ Title = "Green Boxes", Desc = "Top priority (timer)", Type = "Checkbox", Value = S.FarmGreen, Default = S.FarmGreen, Callback = function(v) S.FarmGreen = v end })
UI.FarmGold = FarmTab:Toggle({ Title = "Gold Boxes", Desc = "Second priority (timer) · 5 = triple cash", Type = "Checkbox", Value = S.FarmGold, Default = S.FarmGold, Callback = function(v) S.FarmGold = v end })
UI.FarmNormal = FarmTab:Toggle({ Title = "Normal Boxes", Desc = "While green/gold are on their timers", Type = "Checkbox", Value = S.FarmNormal, Default = S.FarmNormal, Callback = function(v) S.FarmNormal = v end })
FarmTab:Space()
FarmTab:Section({ Title = "Movement", TextSize = 14 })
UI.FarmCar = FarmTab:Dropdown({
    Title = "Farm Car (locked)", Desc = "The ONLY car the farm uses (swaps your other car out if needed)",
    Values = FarmCar.values(), Value = FarmCar.value(),
    Callback = function(v) FarmCar.set(v, "FarmCar") end,
})
UI.FarmUseCar = FarmTab:Toggle({ Title = "Use Car When Far", Desc = "Spawns/uses your car for everything (boxes, mop job, mop shop) · glide speed is locked at 20", Type = "Checkbox", Value = S.FarmUseCar, Default = S.FarmUseCar, Callback = function(v) S.FarmUseCar = v end })
FarmTab:Paragraph({ Title = "Drive Mode", Desc = "🔒 Above ground (dodges walls, drives through them if it has to) · underground doesn't work anymore" })
UI.FarmCarDist = FarmTab:Slider({
    Title = "Use Car If Farther Than", Desc = "Studs from the gas station",
    Step = 25, Value = { Min = 50, Max = 1000, Default = S.FarmCarDist },
    Callback = function(v) S.FarmCarDist = tonumber(v) or 250 end,
})
UI.FarmDelay = FarmTab:Slider({
    Title = "Wait After Each Box", Desc = "Seconds",
    Step = 0.5, Value = { Min = 0, Max = 5, Default = S.FarmDelay },
    Callback = function(v) S.FarmDelay = tonumber(v) or 0.5 end,
})
FarmTab:Space()
FarmTab:Button({
    Title = "Copy Farm Info", Desc = "Copies the farm state (send it to me if something's off)", Icon = "clipboard",
    Callback = function()
        if Farm.dump() then notify("Farm", "Farm info copied", "check") else notify("Farm", "Your executor can't copy", "x") end
    end,
})
task.spawn(function()
    while ALIVE() do
        task.wait(1.5)
        pcall(function() farmPara:SetDesc(farmStatus()) end)
    end
end)

-- ── MOP ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
local MopTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "Mop", Icon = "sparkles" })
local MOP_CHOICES = { "Emerald Mop ($1500 · +50% cash)", "Golden Mop ($1000 · +30% cash)" }
local function mopChoiceName(v)
    if typeof(v) == "table" then v = v.Title or v[1] end
    return string.find(tostring(v), "Golden") and "GoldenMop" or "EsmeraldMop"
end
local function mopStatus()
    local own = {}
    for _, n in ipairs(Mop.PRIORITY) do if Mop.has(n) then own[#own+1] = Mop.MOPS[n].label end end
    local best = Mop.bestOwned()
    local d, n = Mop.puddleCount()
    local s = "Mops: " .. (#own > 0 and table.concat(own, ", ") or "none yet")
        .. "   Cash bonus: " .. (best and ("x" .. Mop.MOPS[best].mult) or "x1")
        .. "\nPuddles: " .. d .. " dirty / " .. n
        .. "\nCleaned: " .. Mop.cleaned .. "   Earned: $" .. Mop.earned
    if Mop.lastNotify ~= "" then s = s .. "\nJob: " .. Mop.lastNotify end
    return s .. "\nMop Farm: " .. Mop.status
end
local mopPara = MopTab:Paragraph({ Title = "Mop Job", Desc = "Scanning…" })
MopTab:Space()
UI.MopFarm = MopTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Farm Mop", Desc = "Drives to the restaurant, takes the mop job, cleans every puddle, repeats (noclip is always on)",
    Type = "Checkbox", Value = S.MopFarm, Default = S.MopFarm,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v and S.FishFarm then Fish.stop(); pcall(function() UI.FishFarm:Set(false) end) end
        if v and S.BothFarm then Both.stop(); pcall(function() UI.BothFarm:Set(false) end) end
        if v and S.FishBoxFarm then FB.stop(); pcall(function() UI.FishBoxFarm:Set(false) end) end
        S.MopFarm = v
        if v and S.BoxFarm then Farm.stop(); pcall(function() UI.BoxFarm:Set(false) end) end
        if v then Mop.start(notify) else Mop.stop(); pcall(function() if Travel.isBusy() then Travel.cancel() end end) end
    end,
})
MopTab:Space()
MopTab:Section({ Title = "Mop Shop", TextSize = 14 })
UI.MopBuy = MopTab:Dropdown({
    Title = "Mop To Buy", Desc = "Better mop = more money at the restaurant",
    Values = MOP_CHOICES, Value = S.MopBuy == "GoldenMop" and MOP_CHOICES[2] or MOP_CHOICES[1],
    Callback = function(v) S.MopBuy = mopChoiceName(v) end,
})
MopTab:Button({
    Title = "Buy Mop Now", Desc = "Drives to the mop shop and buys the mop you picked", Icon = "shopping-cart",
    Callback = function()
        if Mop.isBuying() then return notify("Mop", "Already buying…", "x") end
        task.spawn(function() Mop.buy(S.MopBuy, notify) end)
    end,
})
UI.MopAutoBuy = MopTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Buy Before Farming", Desc = "If you don't own the mop you picked, buys it first (spends your cash)",
    Type = "Checkbox", Value = S.MopAutoBuy, Default = S.MopAutoBuy,
    Callback = function(v) S.MopAutoBuy = v end,
})
MopTab:Space()
MopTab:Button({
    Title = "Copy Mop Info", Desc = "Copies the mop job state (send it to me if something's off)", Icon = "clipboard",
    Callback = function()
        if Mop.dump() then notify("Mop", "Mop info copied", "check") else notify("Mop", "Your executor can't copy", "x") end
    end,
})
Mop.scan()
task.spawn(function()
    while ALIVE() do
        task.wait(1.5)
        pcall(function() mopPara:SetDesc(mopStatus()) end)
    end
end)

-- ── FISHING ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
do
    local FishTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "Fish", Icon = "fish" })
    local function rodLabel(n) for _, r in ipairs(Fish.RODS) do if r.name == n then return r.label end end return Fish.RODS[2].label end
    local function baitLabel(n) for _, b in ipairs(Fish.BAITS) do if b.name == n then return b.label end end return Fish.BAITS[1].label end
    local function pick(list, v)
        if typeof(v) == "table" then v = v.Title or v[1] end
        for _, it in ipairs(list) do if it.label == v then return it.name end end
        return nil
    end
    local function labels(list) local t = {} for _, it in ipairs(list) do t[#t + 1] = it.label end return t end
    local function fishStatus()
        if not Fish.available then return "❌ Fishing not found in this game" end
        local rod = Fish.rodTool()
        local zn = Fish.zoneN()
        local _, _, _, src = Fish.zoneSpot(zn)
        local want = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(S.FishZone) or 1), 1, #Fish.ZONES)
        local s = "Rod: " .. (rod and rod.Name or "none (bought at the shop)")
            .. "   Bait left: " .. Fish.baitTotal()
            .. "\nSpot: level " .. Fish.ZONES[zn].lvl .. (zn ~= want and (" (level " .. Fish.ZONES[want].lvl .. " locked)") or "") .. "   You: lvl " .. tostring(Fish.myLevel() or "?")
            .. "   Spot: " .. (src == "yours" and "yours" or src == "found" and "found" or src == "pier" and "pier" or "not set")
            .. "   Bag: " .. Fish.bagCount() .. "/18"
            .. "\nCar park: 🔒 on the ramp, behind the end"
            .. "   Sell drive: " .. (Fish.isRecording() and "recording…" or (select(2, Fish.sellRoute()) and "yours" or "built-in"))
            .. "\nCycles: " .. Fish.cycles .. "   Caught: " .. Fish.caught .. "   Fish value: $" .. Fish.earned .. "   Sold: $" .. Fish.sold
        if Fish.lastFish ~= "" then s = s .. "\nLast fish: " .. Fish.lastFish end
        return s .. "\nAuto Fish: " .. (S.FishFarm and Fish.status or "Off")
    end
    local fishPara = FishTab:Paragraph({ Title = "Fishing", Desc = "Scanning…" })
    FishTab:Space()
    UI.FishFarm = FishTab:Toggle({
        Title = "Auto Fish", Desc = "Buys rod + bait, walks out to the water on the pier, fishes until the bait is gone, drives to the seller and sells from the car, drives back, repeats",
        Type = "Checkbox", Value = S.FishFarm, Default = S.FishFarm,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then
                if S.BothFarm then Both.stop(); pcall(function() UI.BothFarm:Set(false) end) end
                if S.FishBoxFarm and not FB.isRunning() then FB.stop(); pcall(function() UI.FishBoxFarm:Set(false) end) end
                if S.BoxFarm then Farm.stop(); pcall(function() UI.BoxFarm:Set(false) end) end
                if S.MopFarm then Mop.stop(); pcall(function() UI.MopFarm:Set(false) end) end
                S.FishFarm = true
                Fish.start(notify)
            else
                Fish.stop()
                pcall(function() if Travel.isBusy() then Travel.cancel() end end)
            end
        end,
    })
    UI.FishCastPower = FishTab:Slider({
        Title = "Cast Power", Desc = "How far Auto Fish casts — 10 = held till the bar is RED (farthest, better fish). Also used by the Fish test button",
        Step = 1, Value = { Min = 1, Max = 10, Default = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(S.FishCastPower) or 10), 1, 10) },
        Callback = function(v) S.FishCastPower = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(v) or 10), 1, 10) end,
    })
    local function zoneLabel(n)
        local z = Fish.ZONES[n]
        return z.lvl > 1 and ("Level " .. z.lvl .. " spot (area " .. n .. ")") or "Level 1 spot (pier, everyone)"
    end
    local zoneVals = {}
    for n = 1, #Fish.ZONES do zoneVals[n] = zoneLabel(n) end
    UI.FishZone = FishTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "Fishing Level", Desc = "Which fishing area to fish at — only works if you've unlocked it (falls back to the best one you have)",
        Values = zoneVals, Value = zoneLabel(math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(S.FishZone) or 1), 1, #Fish.ZONES)),
        Callback = function(v)
            if typeof(v) == "table" then v = v.Title or v[1] end
            for n = 1, #Fish.ZONES do
                if v == zoneVals[n] then
                    S.FishZone = n
                    if Fish.isUnlocked(n) == false then
                        notify("Fishing", "The level " .. Fish.ZONES[n].lvl .. " spot is LOCKED (you're level " .. tostring(Fish.myLevel() or "?") .. ") — it'll fish at the level " .. Fish.ZONES[Fish.zoneN()].lvl .. " spot for now", "lock")
                    end
                end
            end
        end,
    })
    FishTab:Space()
    FishTab:Section({ Title = "Rod & Bait", TextSize = 14 })
    UI.FishRod = FishTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "Rod To Buy", Desc = "Rods are for one life — it buys a new one after you die",
        Values = labels(Fish.RODS), Value = rodLabel(S.FishRod),
        Callback = function(v) S.FishRod = pick(Fish.RODS, v) or S.FishRod end,
    })
    UI.FishBuyRod = FishTab:Toggle({
        Title = "Auto Buy Rod", Desc = "No rod -> goes to the fishing shop and buys the one you picked (cheaper one if you can't afford it)",
        Type = "Checkbox", Value = S.FishBuyRod, Default = S.FishBuyRod,
        Callback = function(v) S.FishBuyRod = v end,
    })
    UI.FishBait = FishTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "Bait", Desc = "You NEED bait to fish · better bait = rarer fish (one per cast, it buys more when you run out)",
        Values = labels(Fish.BAITS), Value = baitLabel(S.FishBait),
        Callback = function(v) S.FishBait = pick(Fish.BAITS, v) or S.FishBait end,
    })
    UI.FishBaitAmount = FishTab:Slider({
        Title = "Bait Per Shop Trip", Desc = "How many baits it buys each time",
        Step = 1, Value = { Min = 1, Max = 20, Default = S.FishBaitAmount },
        Callback = function(v) S.FishBaitAmount = tonumber(v) or 5 end,
    })
    FishTab:Button({
        Title = "Go To Fishing Shop", Desc = "Goes to the shop pad and buys a rod (if you have none) + bait (if picked)", Icon = "shopping-cart",
        Callback = function()
            if Fish.isRunning() then return notify("Fishing", "Turn off Auto Fish first", "x") end
            task.spawn(function()
                local go = true
                Fish.shop(function() return go and ALIVE() end, notify, Fish.rodTool() == nil, true)
                Fish.status = "Off"
            end)
        end,
    })
    FishTab:Space()
    FishTab:Section({ Title = "Selling", TextSize = 14 })
    UI.FishAutoSell = FishTab:Toggle({
        Title = "Auto Sell", Desc = "🔒 Drives to the seller and sells everything when your bag is full (18)",
        Type = "Checkbox", Value = S.FishAutoSell, Default = S.FishAutoSell,
        Callback = function(v) S.FishAutoSell = v end,
    })
    task.spawn(function()
        while ALIVE() do
            task.wait(1.5)
            pcall(function() fishPara:SetDesc(fishStatus()) end)
        end
    end)
end

-- ── TRAVEL ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- ================================================================
-- SHOP  (every crate in the game: weapon crates, car crates, car wraps,
--        weapon wraps, gun mod crate)
--   Pick one in the dropdown -> "Buy This Crate" -> it goes to the place,
--   stands on the circle and buys it.
--   Crates  -> CrateRemotes.RequestOpenCrate:FireServer(crateId)   (recorded)
--              CrateZoneEntered/Exited = the SERVER saying you're on a crate circle
--   Wraps   -> WrapRemotes.RequestRoll / RequestWeaponRoll / RequestAttachmentRoll
--              (exact args learned from the game the first time a roll happens,
--               saved to drixnvsp_shop.json; until then it tries the likely formats)
-- ================================================================
local Shop = {}
;(function()   -- own function: keeps the main script under Luau's 200-local limit
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local CR = RS:FindFirstChild("CrateRemotes")
    local WR = RS:FindFirstChild("WrapRemotes")
    local rOpen    = CR and CR:FindFirstChild("RequestOpenCrate")
    local rResult  = CR and CR:FindFirstChild("CrateResult")
    local rZoneIn  = CR and CR:FindFirstChild("CrateZoneEntered")
    local rZoneOut = CR and CR:FindFirstChild("CrateZoneExited")
    local function wr(n) return WR and WR:FindFirstChild(n) end
    Shop.available = rOpen ~= nil
    Shop.tuneAvailable = WR ~= nil

    local function safeRequire(name)
        local m = RS:FindFirstChild(name)
        if not (m and m:IsA("ModuleScript")) then return nil end
        local done, ok, res = false, false, nil
        task.spawn(function() ok, res = pcall(require, m); done = true end)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while not done and os.clock() - t0 < 2 do task.wait(0.05) end
        return (done and ok and type(res) == "table") and res or nil
    end

    -- ── every crate (read from the game's own config) ────────────
    local cfgWrap = safeRequire("WrapConfig")
    local cfgAtt  = safeRequire("AttachmentConfig")
    local cfgWW   = safeRequire("WeaponWrapConfig")
    local cfgCrate = safeRequire("CrateConfig")
    local PRICE = {
        wrap     = (cfgWrap and tonumber(cfgWrap.RollPrice)) or 750,
        wrapPlus = (cfgWrap and tonumber(cfgWrap.PlusRollPrice)) or 2000,
        attach   = (cfgAtt and tonumber(cfgAtt.Price)) or 10000,
    }
    local ITEMS = {}
    if cfgCrate and type(cfgCrate.Crates) == "table" then
        for _, c in ipairs(cfgCrate.Crates) do
            if type(c) == "table" and type(c.Id) == "string" then
                ITEMS[#ITEMS+1] = { id = c.Id, name = tostring(c.Name or c.Id), price = tonumber(c.Price) or 0, zone = tostring(c.Zone or "Weapons"), kind = "crate" }
            end
        end
    end
    if #ITEMS == 0 then
        ITEMS = {
            { id = "WeaponCrateBasic", name = "Basic Crate",   price = 1500, zone = "Weapons", kind = "crate" },
            { id = "WeaponCrateElite", name = "Elite Crate",   price = 6000, zone = "Weapons", kind = "crate" },
            { id = "CarCrateBasic",    name = "Car Crate",     price = 2000, zone = "Car",     kind = "crate" },
            { id = "CarCrateVIP",      name = "VIP Car Crate", price = 6000, zone = "Car",     kind = "crate" },
        }
    end
    if WR then
        ITEMS[#ITEMS+1] = { id = "car_wrap",         name = "Car Wrap",          price = PRICE.wrap,     kind = "car",    plus = false }
        ITEMS[#ITEMS+1] = { id = "car_wrap_plus",    name = "Car Wrap Plus",     price = PRICE.wrapPlus, kind = "car",    plus = true }
        ITEMS[#ITEMS+1] = { id = "weapon_wrap",      name = "Weapon Wrap",       price = PRICE.wrap,     kind = "weapon", plus = false }
        ITEMS[#ITEMS+1] = { id = "weapon_wrap_plus", name = "Weapon Wrap Plus",  price = PRICE.wrapPlus, kind = "weapon", plus = true }
        ITEMS[#ITEMS+1] = { id = "gun_mod",          name = "Gun Mod Crate",     price = PRICE.attach,   kind = "attach", plus = false }
    end
    local WEAPONS = {}
    if cfgWW and type(cfgWW.Weapons) == "table" then
        for _, w in ipairs(cfgWW.Weapons) do
            if type(w) == "table" and type(w.Name) == "string" then WEAPONS[#WEAPONS+1] = w.Name end
        end
    end
    if #WEAPONS == 0 then
        WEAPONS = { "Glock", "DoubleBarril", "Draco", "MP5", "Remington", "AK47", "Shotgun", "RPG", "Anaconda", "FireLauncher", "Sniper3", "M249" }
    end
    Shop.weapons = WEAPONS
    Shop.items = ITEMS

    local function label(it) return it.name .. "  ($" .. it.price .. ")" end
    function Shop.crateById(id)
        for _, it in ipairs(ITEMS) do if it.id == id then return it end end
        return ITEMS[1]
    end
    function Shop.labels()
        local t = {}
        for _, it in ipairs(ITEMS) do t[#t+1] = label(it) end
        return t
    end
    function Shop.label(id) return label(Shop.crateById(id)) end
    function Shop.idFromLabel(l)
        for _, it in ipairs(ITEMS) do if l == label(it) or l == it.id or l == it.name then return it.id end end
        return nil
    end
    local function refreshPrices()
        pcall(function()
            local gui = pg:FindFirstChild("CratesGui")
            local list = gui and gui.Backdrop.MainPanel.ScrollingFrame
            if not list then return end
            for _, it in ipairs(ITEMS) do
                if it.kind == "crate" then
                    local card = list:FindFirstChild("CrateCard_" .. it.id)
                    local btn = card and card:FindFirstChild("Frame") and card.Frame:FindFirstChild("TextButton")
                    local n = btn and tonumber(string.match(btn.Text, "%$(%d+)"))
                    if n then it.price = n end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function cash()
        local st = L:FindFirstChild("Stats")
        local c = st and st:FindFirstChild("Cash")
        if c then return tonumber(c.Value) or 0 end
        local ls = L:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
        c = ls and (ls:FindFirstChild("Cash") or ls:FindFirstChild("Money"))
        return c and tonumber(c.Value) or math.huge
    end
    Shop.cash = cash
    local function myHRP() return L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
    local function myHum() return L.Character and L.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") end

    -- ── learned data (spots + roll formats) ──────────────────────
    local LEARN_FILE = "drixnvsp_shop.json"
    local LEARN = { zones = {}, spots = {}, args = {} }
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and readfile and isfile(LEARN_FILE) then
            local d = Http:JSONDecode(readfile(LEARN_FILE))
            if type(d) == "table" then
                LEARN.zones = type(d.zones) == "table" and d.zones or {}
                LEARN.spots = type(d.spots) == "table" and d.spots or {}
                LEARN.args  = type(d.args) == "table" and d.args or {}
            end
        end
    end)
    local saveQueued = false
    local function saveLearn()
        if saveQueued then return end
        saveQueued = true
        task.delay(1, function()
            saveQueued = false
            pcall(function() if writefile then writefile(LEARN_FILE, Http:JSONEncode(LEARN)) end end)
        end)
    end
    local function v3(t) return (type(t) == "table" and tonumber(t[1])) and Vector3.new(t[1], t[2], t[3]) or nil end
    local function tv(p) return { math.floor(p.X * 10) / 10, math.floor(p.Y * 10) / 10, math.floor(p.Z * 10) / 10 } end

    -- ── the circles (crate zones) + tuning spots, also as they stream in ─
    local zoneParts, tuneParts, tunePrompts = {}, {}, {}
    local function isTuneText(s)
        s = string.lower(s or "")
        return string.find(s, "tun", 1, true) or string.find(s, "wrap", 1, true) or string.find(s, "custom", 1, true)
            or string.find(s, "paint", 1, true) or string.find(s, "attach", 1, true)
    end
    local function consider(d)
        if d:IsA("BasePart") then
            if type(d:GetAttribute("CrateZone")) == "string" then zoneParts[#zoneParts+1] = d end
            local n = string.lower(d.Name)
            if (string.find(n, "tuning", 1, true) or string.find(n, "tunning", 1, true)) and d.Parent and not d.Parent:IsA("Tool") then
                tuneParts[#tuneParts+1] = d
            end
        elseif d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            if isTuneText(d.ActionText) or isTuneText(d.ObjectText) or isTuneText(d.Name) or (d.Parent and isTuneText(d.Parent.Name)) then
                tunePrompts[#tunePrompts+1] = d
            end
        end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local n = 0
        for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            n = n + 1
            if n % 2000 == 0 then task.wait() end
            consider(d)
        end
        workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then consider(d) end
        end)
    end)

    local curZone = nil
    if rZoneIn then
        rZoneIn.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(zone)
            curZone = tostring(zone or "")
            local hrp = myHRP()
            if hrp and curZone ~= "" then LEARN.zones[curZone] = tv(hrp.Position); saveLearn() end
        end)
    end
    if rZoneOut then rZoneOut.OnClientEvent:Connect(function() curZone = nil end) end

    -- circles for a zone, best first (the ones the game names first, then what we learned)
    local ZONE_FALLBACK = { Weapons = Vector3.new(-152.4, 366.3, 684.9) }
    local function zoneSpots(zone)
        local list, seen = {}, {}
        local function push(p)
            if p then
                local k = math.floor(p.X / 4) .. "," .. math.floor(p.Z / 4)
                if not seen[k] then seen[k] = true; list[#list+1] = p end
            end
        end
        local named = {}
        for _, p in ipairs(zoneParts) do
            if p.Parent and p:GetAttribute("CrateZone") == zone then
                local n = string.lower(p.Name)
                if string.find(n, "roll", 1, true) or n == "crates" then table.insert(named, 1, p.Position) else named[#named+1] = p.Position end
            end
        end
        push(v3(LEARN.zones[zone]))
        for _, p in ipairs(named) do push(p) end
        push(ZONE_FALLBACK[zone])
        if zone ~= "Weapons" then
            push(v3(LEARN.zones.Weapons) or ZONE_FALLBACK.Weapons)
            push(Travel.placePos and Travel.placePos("Loc_Dealer"))
        end
        return list
    end

    -- ── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local function moneyMsg(m)
        m = string.lower(m or "")
        return string.find(m, "enough", 1, true) or string.find(m, "money", 1, true) or string.find(m, "cash", 1, true)
            or string.find(m, "dinero", 1, true) or string.find(m, "suficiente", 1, true) or string.find(m, "afford", 1, true)
    end
    local function readResult(a, b)
        if type(a) == "table" then
            local ok = not (a.success == false or a.Success == false or a.ok == false)
            local msg = a.message or a.msg or a.error or a.reason or a.display or a.Display or a.rewardName or a.name or a.id or a.Id or ""
            return ok, tostring(msg), tonumber(a.newCash)
        elseif type(a) == "boolean" then
            return a, tostring(b or ""), nil
        elseif type(a) == "string" then
            local low = string.lower(a)
            if string.find(low, "not ", 1, true) or string.find(low, "fail", 1, true) or moneyMsg(low)
                or string.find(low, "error", 1, true) or string.find(low, "must", 1, true) or string.find(low, "no ", 1, true) then
                return false, a, nil
            end
            return true, a, nil
        end
        return true, "", nil
    end
    local busy = false
    function Shop.isBusy() return busy end
    local function status(ok, msg, c)
        if Shop.onResult then pcall(Shop.onResult, ok, msg or "", c) end
    end
    local function goTo(target, notifyFn, stayInCar)
        local hrp = myHRP()
        if not hrp then return false end
        if (hrp.Position - target).Magnitude > 120 and Travel.trip then
            local fin = false
            Travel.trip(target, nil, notifyFn, not stayInCar, function() fin = true end, stayInCar and { waitAll = true } or nil)
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while not fin and os.clock() - t0 < 180 do task.wait(0.25) end
        end
        if not stayInCar and Travel.glide then Travel.glide(target, function() return not Travel._cancelWait end) end
        return true
    end
    local function standOn(target, secs, untilFn)
        local hrp = myHRP()
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < (secs or 1) do
            if hrp and hrp.Parent then SESSION.tp(hrp, CFrame.new(target) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation, "standOn"); hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
            if untilFn and untilFn() then break end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end
    -- ask the game to refresh the hotbar / weapon bank (the same calls the game makes)
    local function resyncInventory()
        for _, pair in ipairs({ { "HotbarEvents", "RequestSync" }, { "WeaponBankRemotes", "RequestSync" } }) do
            pcall(function()
                local f = RS:FindFirstChild(pair[1])
                local r = f and f:FindFirstChild(pair[2])
                if r then r:FireServer() end
            end)
        end
    end

    -- ================================================================
    -- CRATES: go to the circle, stand on it, buy
    -- ================================================================
    local crateSeq, crateOk, crateMsg = 0, nil, ""
    local function crateAttempt(item)
        local seq0 = crateSeq
        pcall(function() rOpen:FireServer(item.id) end)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while crateSeq == seq0 and os.clock() - t0 < 3 do task.wait(0.05) end
        if crateSeq == seq0 then return false, nil, "" end
        return true, crateOk, crateMsg
    end
    local function buyCrate(item, notifyFn)
        -- already on the right circle -> buy right here
        if curZone == item.zone then
            local g, o, m = crateAttempt(item)
            if g and (o or moneyMsg(m)) then return o, m end
        end
        local g, o, m
        for i, spot in ipairs(zoneSpots(item.zone)) do
            if i > 3 then break end
            notifyFn("Shop", "Going to the " .. item.name .. " circle…", "navigation")
            goTo(spot, notifyFn, false)
            standOn(spot, 2.5, function() return curZone == item.zone end)
            g, o, m = crateAttempt(item)
            if g and o then return true, m end
            if g and moneyMsg(m) then return false, m end
        end
        return false, (g and m ~= "" and m) or "The crate shop didn't answer — try again"
    end
    if rResult then
        rResult.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(res, ...)
            local ok, msg, c = readResult(res, ...)
            if type(res) == "table" and (res.display or res.toolName) then msg = tostring(res.display or res.toolName) end
            crateOk, crateMsg = ok, msg
            crateSeq = crateSeq + 1
            status(ok, (ok and "Got " or "") .. msg, c)
            if ok then task.delay(1.5, resyncInventory); task.delay(6, resyncInventory) end
        end)
    end

    -- ================================================================
    -- WRAPS + GUN MOD CRATE
    -- ================================================================
    local KIND = {
        car    = { req = "RequestRoll",           res = "RollResult",           open = "OpenTuning",       exit = "ExitTuning",       gui = "TuningGui" },
        weapon = { req = "RequestWeaponRoll",     res = "WeaponRollResult",     open = "OpenWeaponTuning", exit = "ExitWeaponTuning", gui = "WeaponTuningGui" },
        attach = { req = "RequestAttachmentRoll", res = "AttachmentRollResult", open = "OpenWeaponTuning", exit = "ExitWeaponTuning", gui = "WeaponTuningGui" },
    }
    local reqToKind = {}
    for k, v in pairs(KIND) do reqToKind[v.req] = k end
    local tuneOpen = { car = false, weapon = false }
    local function guiOpen(name)
        for _, g in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
            if g.Name == name and g:IsA("ScreenGui") and g.Enabled then
                local mp = g:FindFirstChild("MainPanel")
                if mp and mp:IsA("GuiObject") and mp.Visible then return true end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function isOpen(kind)
        return tuneOpen[kind == "car" and "car" or "weapon"] or guiOpen(KIND[kind].gui)
    end
    for _, k in ipairs({ "car", "weapon" }) do
        local o, e = wr(KIND[k].open), wr(KIND[k].exit)
        if o then o.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
            tuneOpen[k] = true
            local hrp, hum = myHRP(), myHum()
            if hrp then LEARN.spots[k] = { pos = tv(hrp.Position), seated = hum and hum.SeatPart ~= nil or false }; saveLearn() end
        end) end
        if e then e.OnClientEvent:Connect(function() tuneOpen[k] = false end) end
    end
    local rseq, rok, rmsg = { car = 0, weapon = 0, attach = 0 }, {}, {}
    for k, v in pairs(KIND) do
        local r = wr(v.res)
        if r then
            r.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
                local ok, msg, c = readResult(...)
                rok[k], rmsg[k] = ok, msg
                rseq[k] = rseq[k] + 1
                status(ok, (k == "car" and "Car wrap: " or k == "weapon" and "Weapon wrap: " or "Gun mod: ") .. msg, c)
            end)
        end
    end
    local function tool(name)
        local ch, bp = L.Character, L:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        return (ch and ch:FindFirstChild(name)) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name))
    end
    local function candidates(kind, plus, weapon)
        local list = {}
        local l = LEARN.args[kind .. (plus and "_plus" or "")]
        if type(l) == "table" and type(l.a) == "table" then
            local a = {}
            for i = 1, (l.n or #l.a) do
                local x = l.a[i]
                if type(x) == "table" and x.tool then x = tool(weapon or x.tool) or weapon or x.tool
                elseif l.w and x == l.w and weapon then x = weapon end
                a[i] = x
            end
            list[#list+1] = { a = a, n = l.n or #l.a, learned = true }
        end
        local W = weapon
        local guesses
        if kind == "car" then
            guesses = plus and { { "wraps_plus" }, { true }, { "plus" } } or { { "wraps" }, { false }, {}, { "normal" } }
        elseif kind == "weapon" then
            guesses = plus and { { W, "wraps_plus" }, { "wraps_plus", W }, { W, true } } or { { W, "wraps" }, { "wraps", W }, { W, false }, { W } }
        else
            guesses = { { W }, {}, { tool(W) } }
        end
        for _, g in ipairs(guesses) do list[#list+1] = { a = g, n = #g } end
        return list
    end
    local function learnFrom(remoteName, args)
        local kind = reqToKind[remoteName]
        if not kind then return end
        local simple, w = {}, nil
        for i = 1, args.n do
            local x = args[i]
            if typeof(x) == "Instance" and x:IsA("Tool") then simple[i] = { tool = x.Name }; w = x.Name
            elseif type(x) == "string" or type(x) == "number" or type(x) == "boolean" then
                simple[i] = x
                if type(x) == "string" then for _, n in ipairs(WEAPONS) do if n == x then w = x end end end
            end
        end
        local cash0 = cash()
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0, delta = os.clock(), 0
            while os.clock() - t0 < 4 do
                task.wait(0.1)
                delta = cash0 - cash()
                if delta > 0 then break end
            end
            local plus = false
            if kind ~= "attach" and delta > 0 then plus = math.abs(delta - PRICE.wrapPlus) < math.abs(delta - PRICE.wrap) end
            if delta > 0 or kind == "attach" then
                LEARN.args[kind .. (plus and "_plus" or "")] = { a = simple, n = args.n, w = w }
                saveLearn()
            end
        end)
    end
    local function rollOnce(kind, argset)
        local r = wr(KIND[kind].req)
        if not r then return false, false, "roll remote missing" end
        local seq0, cash0 = rseq[kind], cash()
        pcall(function() r:FireServer(table.unpack(argset.a, 1, argset.n)) end)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < 2.5 do
            if rseq[kind] ~= seq0 then return true, rok[kind], rmsg[kind] end
            if cash() < cash0 then return true, true, "" end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        return false, nil, ""
    end
    local function tryRoll(kind, plus, weapon)
        local lastMsg = ""
        for _, c in ipairs(candidates(kind, plus, weapon)) do
            local got, ok, msg = rollOnce(kind, c)
            if got and ok then
                if not c.learned then
                    local w
                    local simple = {}
                    for i = 1, c.n do
                        local x = c.a[i]
                        if x == weapon then w = weapon end
                        simple[i] = (typeof(x) == "Instance") and { tool = x.Name } or x
                    end
                    LEARN.args[kind .. (plus and "_plus" or "")] = { a = simple, n = c.n, w = w }
                    saveLearn()
                end
                return true, msg
            end
            if got and moneyMsg(msg) then return false, msg end
            if got and msg ~= "" then lastMsg = msg end
        end
        return false, lastMsg
    end
    local function nearestPrompt(pos, radius)
        local best, bd
        for _, pr in ipairs(tunePrompts) do
            local par = pr.Parent
            local pp = par and (par:IsA("BasePart") and par.Position or (par:IsA("Attachment") and par.WorldPosition))
            if pp and pr.Enabled then
                local d = (pp - pos).Magnitude
                if d < (radius or 30) and (not bd or d < bd) then best, bd = pr, d end
            end
        end
        return best
    end
    local function tuneSpot(kind)
        local k = kind == "car" and "car" or "weapon"
        local s = LEARN.spots[k]
        if type(s) == "table" and v3(s.pos) then return v3(s.pos) end
        if k == "weapon" then
            local disp = workspace:FindFirstChild("TuningWeaponDisplays")
            if disp then
                local ok, cf = pcall(function() return disp:GetPivot() end)
                if ok and cf then return Vector3.new(cf.Position.X, 366.3, cf.Position.Z + 8) end
            end
            return Vector3.new(-166, 366.3, 682)
        end
        for _, p in ipairs(tuneParts) do
            if p.Parent and not string.find(string.lower(p.Name), "camera", 1, true) then return p.Position end
        end
        for _, p in ipairs(tuneParts) do if p.Parent then return p.Position end end
        return Vector3.new(-178.7, 366.3, 685.4)
    end
    local function openTuning(kind, notifyFn)
        if isOpen(kind) then return true end
        local spot = tuneSpot(kind)
        local needCar = kind == "car"
        notifyFn("Shop", needCar and "Driving your car to Tuning…" or "Going to Weapon Tuning…", "navigation")
        goTo(spot, notifyFn, needCar)
        if not needCar then standOn(spot, 2, function() return isOpen(kind) end) end
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < 2 and not isOpen(kind) do task.wait(0.1) end
        if not isOpen(kind) then
            local hrp = myHRP()
            local pr = hrp and nearestPrompt(hrp.Position, 35)
            if pr then firePP(pr) end
            t0 = os.clock()
            while os.clock() - t0 < 3 and not isOpen(kind) do task.wait(0.1) end
        end
        return isOpen(kind)
    end
    local function buyRoll(item, weapon, notifyFn)
        local kind = item.kind
        if kind ~= "car" then
            local t = tool(weapon)
            if not t then return false, "You don't have the " .. tostring(weapon) .. " — pick a gun you own" end
            local hum = myHum()
            if hum and t.Parent ~= L.Character then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t) end); task.wait(0.3) end
        end
        if isOpen(kind) then
            local ok, msg = tryRoll(kind, item.plus, weapon)
            if ok or moneyMsg(msg) then return ok, msg end
        end
        openTuning(kind, notifyFn)
        return tryRoll(kind, item.plus, weapon)
    end

    -- learn the game's roll args (only watches the 3 roll remotes)
    local watch = {}
    for _, v in pairs(KIND) do local r = wr(v.req); if r then watch[r] = v.req end end
    if next(watch) and hookmetamethod and getnamecallmethod then
        learnHook(watch, function(self, ...)
            local nm = watch[self]
            if nm and not checkcaller() and getnamecallmethod() == "FireServer" then
                local args = table.pack(...)
                task.defer(learnFrom, nm, args)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- ================================================================
    -- BUY THIS CRATE
    -- ================================================================
    function Shop.buyItem(id, weapon, notifyFn, done)
        notifyFn = notifyFn or function() end
        local item = Shop.crateById(id)
        if not item then local m = "Pick a crate first"; if done then done(false, m) end return false, m end
        if item.kind == "crate" and not rOpen then local m = "Crate shop not found (wrong game?)"; if done then done(false, m) end return false, m end
        if busy then return false, "Already buying — wait for it to finish" end
        refreshPrices()
        if cash() < item.price then
            local m = "Not enough cash for the " .. item.name .. " ($" .. item.price .. ")"
            if done then done(false, m) end
            return false, m
        end
        busy = true
        Travel._cancelWait = false
        task.spawn(function()
            local okAll, ok, msg = pcall(function()
                if item.kind == "crate" then return buyCrate(item, notifyFn) end
                return buyRoll(item, weapon, notifyFn)
            end)
            busy = false
            if not okAll then ok, msg = false, tostring(ok) end
            if done then pcall(done, ok, msg) end
        end)
        return true, item.name
    end
end)()

    end,

    pcall(function() shopPara:SetDesc(d) end)
end

local TravelTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "Travel", Icon = "car" })
local function travelStatus()
    local t = { "📍 All locations built in" }
    local timer = Travel.spawnTimer and ("\nGame car timer: yes" .. (Travel.timerLeft() > 0 and (" (" .. Travel.timerLeft() .. "s left)") or "") .. (Travel.shouldDespawn() and "" or " → avoided (car reused)")) or ""
    local owned = Travel.ownedCars()
    local carLine = #owned > 0 and ("🚗 Your car: " .. Travel.defaultCar()) or "⚠️ You don't own a car — buy one at the Dealership (farms will glide until then)"
    return table.concat(t, "   ") .. "\n" .. carLine .. timer
end
local travelPara = TravelTab:Paragraph({ Title = "Status", Desc = travelStatus() })
TravelTab:Space()
TravelTab:Section({ Title = "Go To", TextSize = 14 })
for _, p in ipairs(Travel.places) do
    TravelTab:Button({
        Title = "Go To " .. p.name, Desc = "Spawns your car and drives you there", Icon = "navigation",
        Callback = function() Travel.go(p.key, notify) end,
    })
end
TravelTab:Button({
    Title = "Stop Driving", Desc = "Stops the car where it is", Icon = "octagon",
    Callback = function() Travel.cancel() end,
})
